THE SILICON ALTAR CORPUS

Entries: 700. Windows: 7.

The corpus states its own scope and spine. Both are load-bearing: they are
how you tell a genuine gap from something deliberately out of scope.

SCOPE NOTE: T9 State Reconciliation. W3 entries preserved from live L4. W0/W1/W4 entries reconstructed by reverse-parsing the July 9 GENERATED windows. Reconstructed entries carry reconstructed:true; their source_ids are empty because entry-level source links are not rendered into HTML.

FRAMEWORK SPINE: Three-Track Labor System (FW-006). W3 is where all three tracks lock in simultaneously between 1713 and 1718. Track A (Asiento) via SSC 1713 renewal. Track B (Yamasee reclassification) via TC-009 reframe 1717 (existing in v7 timeline). Track C (Transportation Act) via TC-015 through TC-018 sequence 1717 to 1718.

WINDOWS:
  Window 0: The Template (deep time to pre-contact) - 77 entries
  Window 1: Launch Codes (1452 to 1602) - 85 entries
  Window 2: The Corporate Grid (1602 to 1704) - 97 entries
  Window 3: Colonial Consolidation (1652 to 1787) - 69 entries
  Window 4: The Transfer (1787 to 1854) - 72 entries
  Window 5: 75-Year Operation (1854 to 1929) - 188 entries
  Window 6: Digital Migration (1921 to 2029) - 102 entries

Each entry below: [id] window year lane tier [MILESTONE], then title, then
body, then its curated links to other entries and its thread memberships.
Tier A is strongest evidence, E weakest. The links are hand-made by the
operator and are not inferences.

Some entries state their claim in the title and carry no body, because their
detail lives in a dossier. Those show two dossier lines instead:

  framework:   the audit construct this entry belongs to. THIS IS THE
               AUDIT'S OWN ANALYTICAL VOCABULARY, NOT THE HISTORICAL
               RECORD. Where an answer rests on a term named here, say
               whose term it is - it is the audit's reading of the
               evidence, not a fact the sources assert.
  assessment:  how strong the dossier holds the evidence to be, and whether
               the question is settled (Warrant: Closed) or still open
               (Warrant: Open). An open warrant is a live question, and an
               answer resting on one should say so.

A missing body is not a gap in the record. It is where that window put the
detail. Do not report those entries as thin or unsourced.

Most entries also carry a sources line, in the form
[TIER STATUS] source-id url. STATUS is the one that matters:

  live_verified   someone doing this audit OPENED that source and recorded
                  what it says.
  citation_only   the source was identified and NEVER OPENED. The entry tier
                  reflects a judgement about the source, not a reading of it.

These are not the same evidential situation and should not be described as
though they were. An entry at tier A whose sources are all citation_only is
a confident claim resting on unread evidence, and when asked how well
something is evidenced, say so. 443 sources are opened and 138 are not.
Absence of a sources line means the entry carries no source at all.

[E-W0-001-01] W0 ~1100 military TA
Templar military-banking fusion: first documented military-industrial-financial complex
The Templars are simultaneously a military order and a banking system. Roughly 15,000 to 20,000 members at peak. Answer only to the Pope. Model: spiritual authority provides jurisdictional cover for military operations that generate financial flows. This triple structure (spiritual + military + financial) is the architecture every subsequent colonial operation inherits: chartered joint-stock companies (VOC 1602, EIC 1600) fuse the same three functions under merchant-sovereign license.
sources: [A citation_only] src-barber-new-knighthood; [B citation_only] src-goetzmann-money-changes-everything
links: omne-datum-optimum-1139, first-crusade-1095, templar-suppression-1307

[E-W0-001-02] W0 ~1100 records TA MILESTONE
Knights Templar banking network: the V2 upgrade to Islamic V1 Hawala
Formally founded 1119 in Jerusalem. The Templars invent cross-border deposit-and-withdrawal: a pilgrim deposits cash at Temple Church London, receives a letter of credit, withdraws in Jerusalem. They run the French royal treasury for nearly a century and lend to Edward I with interest hidden as 'administrative fees' (5,333 livres, six sous, eight deniers on one loan). By 1300 they are too wealthy for any sovereign to tolerate. The Christian usury prohibition chain (Nicaea 325, Charlemagne 789, Lateran II 1139, Lateran III 1179, Council of Vienne 1311) is nominally intact; the Templar workaround (administrative fees) is the upgrade. V1 (Hawala): trust-based, no interest. V2 (Templar): paper-based, hidden interest.
sources: [A citation_only] src-barber-new-knighthood; [B citation_only] src-goetzmann-money-changes-everything

[E-W0-001-03] W0 ~1100 innovation TA
Letters of credit: financial technology that moves money without moving money
The Templar innovation is the letter of credit: cash-in at Temple Church London, cash-out in Jerusalem, no bullion moves. Every subsequent banking system inherits this. The technology that makes cross-border extraction possible.

[E-W0-003-01] W0 ~1380 population TA MILESTONE
Ottoman Devshirme: the Conversion Template (Technology 9) formalized
Formalized under Sultan Murad I c.1380s. The Devshirme ('collection'): Christian boys age 8 to 20 forcibly taken from Balkan families, converted to Islam, stripped of original identity, languages, and family ties, trained as elite soldiers (Janissaries) or administrators. Janissaries then govern their own people. The captured become the instrument of the captor. Technology 9 (Conversion) at its most explicit. THE CONVERSION CHAIN: Mamluk/Devshirme (HERE) to Fichte 1807 (W4) to Mann 1843 (W4) to Carlisle Indian School 1879 (W5) to Rockefeller GEB 1905 (W5) to compulsory attendance all states 1918 (W5) to Brown vs Board integration mandate plus Reece Committee exposure 1954 (W6). The instrument changes at every step (military conscription, philosophical treatise, state education law, boarding school, industrial foundation, textbook oligopoly, court mandate). The function persists: take the children of the targeted population, process them through a managed system, produce subjects who operate within the Manager's framework.

[E-W0-003-02] W0 ~1380b information TA
European Wild Man tradition (homo sylvestris): the classification's own evidence against itself
Throughout the medieval period, European art, literature, and legal records document 'wild men' (homo sylvestris, wodewose, wilder Mann): hair-covered, feral human figures in European forests. Bernheimer's Wild Men in the Middle Ages (1952, Harvard UP, Tier A) is the definitive academic treatment. These include DOCUMENTED CASES of feral humans found in European forests alongside legends. The tradition runs parallel to (and predates) the civilizational narratives that will later classify non-European peoples as 'savage.' CHAIN: Medieval wild man tradition (HERE) to Petrus Gonsalvus 1537 (W1) to Schott's Physica Curiosa 1662 (W2) to Peter of Hanover 1725 to Linnaeus Homo ferus 1758 (W3). The documentary evidence that Europeans themselves exhibited 'feral' conditions exists INSIDE the same institutional record that will later construct the racial hierarchy. The classification's own evidence against itself predates the classification by centuries.
sources: [A citation_only] src-bernheimer-wild-men https://archive.org/details/wildmeninmiddlea00bern; [A citation_only] src-husband-wild-man-1980; [B citation_only] src-martin-wild-men-moors-1983
links: valladolid-moor-native-seam-1550, linnaeus-systema-1758

[E-W0-005-01] W0 ~1241 financial TB
Kontore: Hanseatic extraterritorial trading outposts within sovereign nations
Permanent trading posts (Kontore) established in London (Steelyard), Bruges, Bergen, Novgorod. Extraterritorial enclaves operating under Hanseatic law inside sovereign nations' territory, each with its own treasury, seal, and code of rules. Prototype for colonial trading factories (VOC/EIC), modern free trade zones, and diplomatic immunity zones. DATE: the Kontore developed across the 13th century and later - Novgorod's Peterhof earliest (early 13th c.), Bruges and Bergen last - and were not founded together in 1241; the 1241 slug marks the League's founding alliance, used here as an identifier only.

[E-W0-006-01] W0 ~1311 financial TB
Atlantic crossing precedent: arrival at a known destination on known currents
This event's claim - that a Mali fleet reached the Americas in 1311 and established a pre-Columbian 'arrival' / navigation precedent - is unsupported by any source. The sole account (al-Umari) states the ships 'never returned', and 'No uncontroversial evidence of pre-Columbian contact between Africa and the Americas has ever been found'. The favorable Canary / North-Equatorial current route is real, but a plausible route is not evidence of a completed crossing. The 'prior arrival voids the Discovery-Doctrine premise' argument is framework speculation contingent on an unproven premise - held, not asserted; it is NOT an evidentiary basis. HELD-NULL: no sourceable basis exists for arrival; reframe or retire.
sources: [B live_verified] src-al-umari-masalik-absar https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abubakari_II
links: mansa-abu-bakr-ii-atlantic-expedition-1311, mansa-musa-ascends-richest-person-in-1312

[E-W0-007-01] W0 ~1241 records TB MILESTONE
Hanseatic League formalizes: parallel northern extraction network
The Lubeck-Hamburg treaty (1241) formalizes what becomes a 200-plus city commercial confederation controlling Baltic and North Sea commerce. Parallel extraction network to the Mediterranean Templar/Lombard/Medici chain. Own legal system (Hanseatic law), own currency standards, own naval military. Monopoly on Baltic grain, fish, timber, furs, wax. When the Hansa weakens (~1400s), Mediterranean banking houses absorb the northern trade routes into the same merchant-banker network (FW-016).

[E-W0-008-01] W0 ~1311 records TB MILESTONE
Mansa Abu Bakr II Atlantic expedition: 2,000 boats via al-Umari testimony
al-Umari's Masalik al-Absar (14th c.) preserves Mansa Musa's 1324 Cairo testimony that his unnamed predecessor sent a fleet into the Atlantic - first 200 ships, then 2,000 - that never returned ('a long time passed before anyone came back. Then one ship returned'). This is the ONLY known account: it is 'not mentioned by other medieval Arab historians or West African oral tradition', and 'No uncontroversial evidence of pre-Columbian contact between Africa and the Americas has ever been found'. The popular name 'Abu Bakr II' is a 19th-century mistranslation (Baron de Slane); 'no such mansa ever reigned', and Musa named only 'the king who preceded me' (scholars suggest Muhammad ibn Qu or Mansa Qu). The testimony is a documented primary; the crossing itself is unproven and disputed - recorded testimony, not established fact.
sources: [B live_verified] src-al-umari-masalik-absar https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abubakari_II
links: mansa-musa-ascends-richest-person-in-1312, atlantic-crossing-precedent-arrival-at-a-1311

[E-W0-009-01] W0 ~1100 information TB
Xi-Maya Long Count / Allodial Time: 26,000-year Precession cycle
The Maya Long Count runs on the ~5,125-year cycle of 13 b'ak'tuns (1 b'ak'tun = 144,000 days; the cycle ended 21 December 2012) - a documented Mesoamerican time-reckoning system (tier B). This is DISTINCT from the ~26,000-year precession of the equinoxes (the axial-precession 'Great Year'), a separate astronomical cycle; the reached Long Count source makes 'no association between the Long Count and any ~26,000-year cycle'. The framework's 'Allodial Time' (V2.1 II.3) rests on the precessional Great Year, NOT on the Long Count - the earlier conflation of the two is corrected here. Managerial Time (Gregorian, debt cycles, quarterly returns) is the accounting frame of the management contract; the framework reading holds at tier C on the accurate figures.
sources: [B live_verified] src-maya-long-count https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mesoamerican_Long_Count_calendar
links: cahokia-at-peak-american-levant-governance-1100

[E-W0-011-01] W0 ~300 Ma counter-move TA MILESTONE
Alleghanian Orogeny: geological title predates every Managerial instrument by 300 million years
The Alleghanian Orogeny welds Laurentia and Gondwana in the Pangaea assembly at approximately 300 million years before present. The North Carolina Appalachians and the Moroccan Anti-Atlas are the same rock formation, separated by later Atlantic rifting. The Spruce Pine pegmatite district (western NC) forms during this orogeny, producing ultra-pure quartz (SiO2) which becomes the source of roughly 70 to 80 percent of the world's semiconductor-grade silicon feedstock. This is the geological Hardware. The Roraima Master Battery anchors the southern terminus. Under V2.1 II.1, the land and its silicon are the Allodial Estate. Every subsequent Software instrument (treaty, charter, patch) runs on this substrate.
sources: [A live_verified] src-alleghanian-orogeny-usgs https://www.usgs.gov/publications/kinematics-late-paleozoic-continental-collision-between-laurentia-and-gondwana

[E-W0-012-01] W0 ~1100 counter-move TB MILESTONE
Cahokia at peak: American Levant governance concurrent with Templar formation
Cahokia (present-day East St. Louis, IL), at its peak c. 1050-1150, was 'the largest urban center north of the great Mesoamerican cities in Mexico' - a major Mississippian city and political center whose population is estimated 'between 6,000 and 40,000 at its peak' (at the high estimates, larger than any subsequent city in what became the United States until the 1780s), dominated by Monks Mound (a ten-story platform mound). The documented archaeology is held at tier B. The framework reading - Cahokia as 'American Levant governance' / the Hardware side of the Managerial-Allodial split, concurrent with Templar formation in Europe - is the audit's interpretive overlay (tier C), not a claim of the archaeology.

[E-W0-013-01] W0 338K BP population TA MILESTONE
A00 Y-chromosome: biological title with 338,000-year documented presence
Mendez et al. (2013) document the A00 Y-chromosome haplogroup with a time-to-most-recent-common-ancestor estimate of 338,000 years, 95 per cent confidence interval 237,000 to 581,000 years. THE ESTIMATE IS DISPUTED AND THE CORPUS HOLDS EVERY SIDE OF IT: Elhaik et al. (2014) challenge the substitution rate and revise the figure to 208,300 years, 95 per cent CI 163,900 to 260,200; Mendez et al. reply defending the method; and Thomas Krahn, A00's original discoverer and a co-author on the 2013 paper, is on record that his own figure was closer to 200,000 years. The lineage was discovered in a commercial database of consumer samples from African Americans, and the paper's acknowledgments thank the descendants of Albert Perry of South Carolina. A00 predates the conventional out-of-Africa modern human timeline. Under V2.1 II.1, A00 is the biological Hardware anchor of the Xi-Maya/Moorish Heir lineage. Nemo dat quod non habet: no subsequent Managerial classification (Noun-to-Adjective operation under II.4) can extinguish a lineage that predates the classification apparatus by hundreds of thousands of years on every estimate in the dispute, including the sceptic's floor. Currently maintained as reference haplogroup in academic and commercial DNA databases.
sources: [A live_verified] src-a00-mendez-2013 https://www.cell.com/fulltext/S00029297(13)00073-6; [A citation_only] src-a00-elhaik-2014 https://doi.org/10.1038/ejhg.2013.303; [A citation_only] src-a00-mendez-2014-reply https://doi.org/10.1038/ejhg.2014.148; [B citation_only] src-a00-elhaik-2014-reply2 https://arxiv.org/abs/1410.3972

[E-W0-014-05] W0 711 to 1248 counter-move TA MILESTONE
Islamic banking: the competing riba-prohibited financial architecture
While Templars invent debt-based cross-border banking, Islamic finance operates a parallel system prohibiting riba (usury/interest). Hawala transfers money across continents without ledger debt (trust-based, no central clearinghouse). Mudarabah (profit-sharing), Murabaha (cost-plus), Waqf (charitable endowment as perpetual trust). Cordoba, Baghdad, Cairo run the world's largest economies on a financial architecture that does not require debt issuance. Templars arguably borrow the letter-of-credit concept from Islamic merchants encountered in the Levant, then strip out the usury prohibition. The debt-based model must destroy the alternative because the alternative proves extraction is not the only option.

[E-W0-015-03] W0 1066 records TA MILESTONE
Jewish financiers arrive with the Norman Conquest as designated intermediary
William the Conqueror imports Jewish financiers from Rouen after 1066. Under English royal charter they become 'direct subjects of the King': not protected by baronial charters, forbidden to own land or join guilds, permitted only the money-lending function that Church canon law forbids to Christians. The Crown then taxes the profits at will. Between 1219 and 1272, 49 separate levies extract roughly 200,000 marks from the Anglo-Jewish community. This is the Staffing Agency model: the intermediary collects interest from the population; the Crown taxes it from the intermediary.

Pope Urban II preaches the First Crusade at Clermont in November 1095. Between 60,000 and 100,000 crusaders march east; Jerusalem falls in July 1099 with the mass killing of its Muslim and Jewish population. The operational template is: Papal authority (spiritual instrument) authorizes military conquest (kinetic) to seize territory (geographic) and resources (financial). The same spiritual-to-military-to-financial pipeline will be redeployed on the American Levant via Dum Diversas (1452, W1). The Crusades are the rehearsal.

[E-W0-019-01] W0 1215 population TB MILESTONE
Fourth Lateran Council: classification formalized as ecclesiastical instrument
Pope Innocent III convenes the Fourth Lateran Council November 1215. Canon 68 requires distinguishing dress (badges) for Jews and Muslims. Canons 67 through 70 restrict Jewish economic function and public office. The Church formalizes the claim that 'Christian resources are being siphoned by Jewish usury,' creating the legal architecture for the extraction cycle: MARK to RESTRICT to EXTRACT to BLAME. The badge requirement is exported to England by 1218. Under V2.1 II.4 Software Neutrality, this is Software installation on a designated population category, not race.

King John Lackland signs Magna Carta at Runnymede, June 15, 1215. The charter establishes baronial rights against the Crown. [E-W0-024-01] W0 1275 records TB MILESTONE
Edward I Statute of Jewry: the extraction template begins (PERMIT phase closes, TAX phase opens)
The Statute of Jewry (1275) outlaws all lending at interest. Jews over age seven must wear an identifying badge (double stone tablets, 6 by 3 inches, on the outer garment). Jews must live in designated towns only. Trade requires the King's permission. This is the Extraction Template opening: PERMIT (1066 Norman Conquest) closes, TAX phase intensifies, CONFISCATE (1278) and EXPEL (1290) queue up, REPLACE (1290b Lombards) follows. Under V2.1 II.5, this is Phase 3 Classification firing before Phase 6 Maintenance operations enable confiscation.

[E-W0-026-01] W0 1278 military TB
300 executed in London: violence as spectacle before confiscation
The public execution of English Jews for coin-clipping (1278-79): 269 hanged in London (Rokeah; Medievalists.net), with more than 50 more in other towns - the sources range from 269 to over 300 - against only 29 Christians executed for the same offence. The population learns what happens to the designated intermediary when the Crown decides to collect. The spectacle dresses the seizure as justice for coin-clipping rather than administering it as tax: a Phase 5 Obfuscation precursor before confiscation.

[E-W0-027-01] W0 1278 records TB
Coin-clipping accusations: create the crime, then confiscate
The Crown accuses the Jews of debasing the currency by clipping coins - a capital offence since the 1275 Statute of the Jewry. On 17 November 1278 every Jew in England is arrested; more than 1,178 are held across the realm, as many as 600 in the Tower of London. In 1278-79, 269 Jews are hanged in London (Rokeah; Medievalists.net) with more than 50 executed in other towns - the figures range from 269 to over 300; only 29 Christians are executed for the same offence. The Crown confiscates the estates of the condemned: the fiscal motive (fines, forfeitures, confiscations) is prior, the coin-clipping charge instrumental - create the crime, then confiscate. The same logic recurs in the heresy charges against the Templars (1307) and the vagrancy laws against freedmen (1866, Black Codes).

[E-W0-030-01] W0 1291 records TB
Swiss Confederation founded: possible Templar-personnel connection
The Federal Charter of 1291 (a treaty of alliance between Uri, Schwyz, and Unterwalden, dated early August 1291 - the origin of Swiss National Day) is the documented founding of the Old Swiss Confederacy (tier B). NOTE: its status AS the founding document is itself a modern (1889) historiographical designation; the older traditional date was 1307. The Templar-personnel claim - that suppressed Templars (post-1307) resettled in Switzerland and seeded its decentralized banking - is speculative and unsupported: the reached source makes 'no mention whatsoever of the Knights Templar', and chronologically the Templar suppression (1307-1314) POSTDATES the 1291 founding, so it cannot have founded the Confederation. Held at tier C-speculative, explicitly unproven; the documented decentralized cantonal sovereignty stands on its own.

[E-W0-031-03] W0 1307 records TA MILESTONE
Friday the 13th: Templar suppression solves the too-wealthy-creditor problem
Friday October 13, 1307. Philip IV of France, hopelessly indebted to the Templars, orders the simultaneous arrest of every Templar in France. He coerces Pope Clement V into seizing all Templar assets across Europe. Charged with heresy. Grand Master Jacques de Molay burned alive (1314). Order dissolved by Vox in Excelso (Council of Vienne, 1312). The too-wealthy-creditor problem solved by criminalizing the creditor and seizing the bank. [E-W0-031-05] W0 1307 innovation TA

Open question: the Templar fleet possessed the most advanced navigational capacity in the Mediterranean. Portuguese maritime expansion begins almost immediately after Templar suppression. Prince Henry the Navigator's School of Sagres draws on knowledge that appears to predate 'official' Portuguese exploration. The Knights of Christ (Portugal), reconstituted 1319, are the direct successor order to the Templars, retaining Templar assets. Henry the Navigator is later Grand Master. The navigational knowledge that 'discovers' the Atlantic routes may originate with the suppressed Templar fleet. [E-W0-033-01] W0 1312 records TB MILESTONE
Mansa Musa ascends: richest person in recorded history at the moment of Templar dissolution
Mansa Musa reigns Mali 1312 to 1337. His 1324 Hajj to Mecca distributes so much gold that he crashes the Egyptian gold market for a decade. The same year the Templars are dissolved by Vox in Excelso (1312), the wealthiest sovereign on Earth is African. Mali controls trans-Saharan gold and salt trade. Timbuktu: manuscripts, universities, libraries. The Noun governs the largest economy on Earth while the Managers are fighting over the scraps of the Templar suppression. Under V2.1 II.1, this is Hardware operating at scale outside the European Managerial system.

[E-W0-034-01] W0 1332 information TB MILESTONE
Ibn Khaldun born: the first forensic auditor produces the framework 600 years ahead
Ibn Khaldun (1332 to 1406), Tunis. Moorish/North African polymath. Writes the Muqaddimah (1377), the first systematic analysis of how civilizations rise and fall through cycles of group solidarity (asabiyyah), luxury decay, and institutional capture. He DIAGNOSES THE DISEASE before it is deployed on the Americas: administrative systems are captured by elites who extract wealth until the system collapses; new elites then install a new extraction layer on the same territory. The Khaldunian cycle IS the Siphon pattern. A Moorish scholar, the Noun, produces the analytical framework that predicts the next 600 years of the Managerial lanes. The Heirs did not just have advanced civilization; they had already AUDITED the machine that would attack them.

[E-W0-035-01] W0 1332 innovation TB
Muqaddimah as predictive framework: four-generation cycle applied to every subsequent extractive institution
Ibn Khaldun's model: founding generation has solidarity (asabiyyah), second inherits but does not build, third lives on institutional inertia, fourth collapses. Apply to Templar rise/suppression (four generations, 1119 to 1312), Medici rise/collapse (four generations, 1397 to 1494), and the American Republic to Corporation to Collection Engine to insolvency (four phases, 1776 to 2026). The Moorish intellectual tradition already contains the audit. The nine-lane timeline (W0 through W6) is a Khaldunian document.

[E-W0-036-01] W0 1347 military TA
Black Death as HARDWARE RESET: 75-200 million dead across Eurasia
1347 to 1351. Bubonic plague. 75 to 200 million dead across Eurasia. Jewish communities scapegoated, accused of poisoning wells. Strasbourg massacre (1349): 2,000 Jews burned alive. The plague is natural; the response is administrative. Crisis triggers the extraction cycle: blame the intermediary then confiscate. This is the canonical Phase 0 Trigger of the FW-007 Administrative Patch Cycle.

[E-W0-036-03] W0 1347 records TA MILESTONE
European economy collapses: labor shortage inverts power, banking houses consolidate
The Black Death (1347 to 1351) kills approximately 30 to 60 percent of Europe. Labor shortage inverts power dynamics temporarily. Serfdom weakens in Western Europe. Statute of Labourers (1349) attempts to freeze wages at pre-plague levels, criminalizing refusal to work at pre-plague rates. But banking houses that survive the plague emerge stronger: less competition, more consolidation. This is the trigger event for the FW-007 Administrative Patch Cycle inaugurated in the framework and the first documented data-layer instance of FW-006 Vagrancy Chain (Statute of Labourers is the direct ancestor of the 1824 Vagrancy Act still enforced in England and Wales).

[E-W0-037-01] W0 1349 legal TA MILESTONE
Statute of Labourers 1349: first data-layer instance of the Vagrancy Chain
Edward III's Ordinance of Labourers (1349) followed by the Statute of Labourers (1351) freezes wages at pre-plague levels and criminalizes refusal to work at those rates. Any able-bodied worker who declines pre-plague-rate work may be arrested and forced to labor. This is the first data-layer instance of FW-006 Vagrancy Chain, directly ancestral to the 1824 Vagrancy Act (still enforced in England and Wales), the 1834 Poor Law Amendment, the American Black Codes (1865-66), and modern workfare and gig-economy independent-contractor classification. Under V2.1 II.4 Software Neutrality, the code operates on a jurisdictional category (able-bodied unemployed); its later racial appearance is downstream of the operation.

[E-W0-038-01] W0 1367 records TB
Hans Fugger arrives in Augsburg: banking succession node initialized
Hans Fugger, weaver and small trader, arrives in Augsburg 1367. His descendants control approximately 2 percent of European GDP at peak and buy the election of Holy Roman Emperor Charles V (1519). The Fugger dynasty begins in textiles (same as Medici). The banking succession Templar to Lombard to Medici to Fugger initializes here. Each node inherits the technology and the Vatican relationship. FW-016 Merchant-Banker Constant reads this succession as personnel changing while the extraction architecture persists.

[E-W0-039-01] W0 1397 records TB MILESTONE
Medici Bank founded: papal-treasury banking succession node
Giovanni di Bicci de' Medici founds the Medici Bank in Florence, 1397. Within 25 years the Medici become Depositor of the Apostolic Chamber (papal treasury). Innovations: double-entry bookkeeping perfected, holding-company structure (separate legal entities for each branch), bills of exchange standardized. By 1420: 100,000 florins on deposit from the Papal Curia; the bank's own capital only 25,000. The Vatican's money dwarfs the bank's capital. FW-016 Merchant-Banker Constant node.

Double-entry bookkeeping perfected: the technology that makes the Siphon auditable
The Medici do not invent double-entry (Lombards, c.1300); they perfect it. Every debit has a credit. Every transaction is visible. The technology that makes the Siphon auditable and therefore controllable. Direct ancestor of GAAP, IFRS, and every modern financial reporting framework. The audit tool is also the compliance tool.

You cannot convert out of the Adjective. This is the prototype for the racial classification that the Asiento (1518, W1) will commercialize and Plecker's Racial Integrity Act (1924, W5) will automate. Three years before Dum Diversas (1452, W1).

[E-W1-001-01] W1 1452 population TA
Authorizes perpetual enslavement of "Saracens, pagans, and other unbelievers"
Nicholas V issues Dum Diversas on 18 June 1452, granting Alfonso V of Portugal and his successors full and free faculty to invade, conquer, subdue and reduce to perpetual servitude the Saracens, pagans, 'and any other unbelievers and enemies of Christ wheresoever placed' (aliosque infideles, et Christi inimicos quoscunque), and to seize their kingdoms, possessions and goods and appropriate them to the Portuguese crown. The operative category is defined by jurisdiction to Christendom, not by race or geography, three years before the Romanus Pontifex (1455) recital of the same grant. It is the foundational instrument of the Discovery-Doctrine legal architecture.

[E-W1-002-01] W1 1453 military TB
Fall of Constantinople: marker, not forcing function, of the Atlantic turn
Mehmed II captures Constantinople on May 29, 1453, ending the Byzantine Empire. The event is conventionally read as the forcing function of the Atlantic turn, on the theory that the conquest closed the eastern trade routes. The economic record does not support that reading: Levant prices show no marked shock after 1453, the eastern routes were not in fact closed, and the Iberian maritime search predated the conquest and proceeded from independent motives (Portugal was already pushing south along Africa before 1453). 1453 is a political and symbolic marker within a maritime pivot already underway on its own economics (Lybyer 1915), not its cause.
sources: [A live_verified] src-lybyer-oriental-trade-routes-1915 https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/50860/pg50860.txt; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-fall-of-constantinople https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fall_of_Constantinople

[E-W1-004-02] W1 1455 records TB
Papal succession: doctrine continues through Calixtus III
Nicholas V issues Romanus Pontifex (8 January 1455), then dies in March 1455. Calixtus III succeeds (April 1455) and in 1456 issues his own bull, Inter Caetera (not to be confused with the 1493 bull of the same name), reaffirming Dum Diversas (1452) and Romanus Pontifex (1455) and granting the Portuguese the military Order of Christ under Henry the Navigator, with spiritual jurisdiction over the discovered lands. The successor-pope pattern is the point: papal bulls are institutional software that survives the individual pope's death, reconfirmed across successions.

[E-W1-005-02] W1 1478 legal TB MILESTONE
Spanish Inquisition: FW-019 template redeployment inside Iberia
Sixtus IV issues the bull Exigit Sincerae Devotionis Affectus on 1 November 1478, at Ferdinand and Isabella's request, establishing a royal-controlled Inquisition in Castile (operational launch 1480, first inquisitors Morillo and San Martin at Seville). Its jurisdiction is baptized Christians suspected of heresy - conversos (converted Jews suspected of secret judaizing) and equally Moriscos (converts from Islam) - a religion-defined legal category, not race, run identically on the two converted populations (Software-Neutrality, tagged on the sourced category). But the Inquisition is also the religion-to-blood seam: in operation, limpieza de sangre / purity-of-blood (old-Christian status) becomes the marker separating trustworthy from suspect Christians - the descent-defined classification the framework locates at Toledo 1449 and that hardens downstream into casta. It redeploys the FW-019 suppression template inside Iberia.

[E-W1-006-01] W1 1479 legal TA
Alcacovas: pre-1493 partition of the Atlantic
Signed September 4, 1479 between Castile and Portugal. Ends the War of the Castilian Succession. Establishes the first sovereign partition of Atlantic maritime space: Portugal receives exclusive rights to all discoveries south of the Canaries; Castile retains the Canaries. The proto-Tordesillas division. Papal ratification via Aeterni Regis bull 1481 (Sixtus IV).

Reconquest of Granada: 781-year Iberian phase of FW-020 completes
January 2, 1492. Boabdil surrenders the Emirate of Granada to Ferdinand and Isabella. The 781-year Iberian phase of FW-020 (from 711 al-Andalus founding) completes. [E-W1-007-02] W1 1492a counter-move TA
Treaty of Granada 1491 promises religious protection; broken within a decade
The Capitulaciones de Granada (signed 25 Nov 1491; surrender 2 Jan 1492) guaranteed the religion-defined 'Muslim' population its faith and mosques, its own law under its qadis ('juzgados en sus leyes ... por el derecho del xara'), its property, safe passage to Berberia, and freedom from forced conversion ('ningun moro ni mora seran apremiados a ser cristianos contra su voluntad'). Those sovereign guarantees were administratively reversed within a decade: Cisneros's forced conversions and manuscript burnings, the 1499-1501 Alpujarras revolts, then expulsion. The forensic point is sovereign-guarantee-then-breach - a treaty conferred standing on a religion-defined population, and later provision stripped it. The category is religion/jurisdiction-defined, not race: the Software-Neutrality class of the Alhambra Decree and Code Noir.
sources: [A live_verified] src-treaty-of-granada-1491 https://es.wikisource.org/wiki/Tratado_de_Granada
links: alhambra-decree-1492, toledo-blood-purity-1449
threads: T-SOFTWARE-NEUTRALITY

Alhambra Decree: Iberian expulsion, Sephardic diaspora, Amsterdam capitalization
Ferdinand and Isabella sign the Alhambra Decree at Granada on March 31, 1492, three months after the fall of Granada. All Jews required to convert or leave Spain by July 31 (later extended to August 2). Approximately 200,000 Sephardic Jews expelled or forcibly converted. Portuguese expulsion follows 1497.

[E-W1-009-02] W1 1492c land TA MILESTONE
Columbus 1492: physical Hardware contact predates Software authorization
Columbus departed Palos on 3 August 1492 and made landfall at Guanahani (San Salvador) on 12 October 1492, under Castilian sponsorship. The load-bearing instrument is prior: the Capitulations of Santa Fe (17 April 1492) were a pre-voyage royal patent granting Columbus the admiral/viceroy/governor titles over any lands discovered, one-tenth of all riches, heritable in perpetuity - title and property issued before any land was reached (documented, tier B). The audit's framework reading (tier C, inference, not quoted from the Capitulations): this is discovery-as-title-laundering - a European patent manufacturing title to inhabited sovereign land before contact - whose one-continent-model suppression is later completed at the representational layer by Waldseemuller's 1507 naming. The papal bulls Inter Caetera (1493) and Tordesillas (1494) then ratify the secular pre-grant.

[E-W1-010-01] W1 1493 legal TA MILESTONE
Inter Caetera 1493: the Discovery Doctrine kernel installed
Alexander VI (Rodrigo Borgia) issues Inter Caetera on May 3-4, 1493. Grants Spain all lands 100 leagues west and south of the Azores and Cape Verde, not already occupied by any Christian prince. This is the specific papal-bull software cited 330 years later by Chief Justice John Marshall in Johnson v M'Intosh (1823) to install the Discovery Doctrine into US federal law.

[E-W1-011-01] W1 1494 legal TA MILESTONE
Tordesillas: bilateral treaty carves the Hardware between two crowns
Spain and Portugal negotiate Treaty of Tordesillas at Tordesillas, Castile, signed June 7, 1494. Meridian 370 leagues west of Cape Verde Islands divides the world into Spanish (west) and Portuguese (east) hemispheres. Papal ratification 1506 (Julius II). Consequence: Portugal receives Brazil (which lies east of the line) despite not yet knowing Brazil exists (formally claimed 1500 by Cabral).

[E-W1-012-01] W1 1496 legal TB
Cabot patent 1496: English wedge against Iberian monopoly
Henry VII grants John Cabot (Giovanni Caboto) letters patent on March 5, 1496 authorizing westward exploration on behalf of the English crown. First Northern European challenge to the Spanish-Portuguese Atlantic monopoly established by Alcacovas 1479, Inter Caetera 1493, and Tordesillas 1494. Cabot voyages 1497 and 1498; second voyage disappears.

English challenge to Spanish-Portuguese monopoly begins
5 March 1496: Henry VII's letters patent authorize Cabot to find and claim, for the English Crown, lands 'which before this time were unknown to all Christians' - the land-claim instrument of the English Discovery-Doctrine wedge, paralleling the Iberian bulls (Inter Caetera 1493, Tordesillas 1494).

[E-W1-013-02] W1 1503 legal TA MILESTONE
Encomienda 1503: FW-020 Reconquista template exported to the Americas
Forced labor is ordered in the 1501 Ovando instructions ('require the Indians to work in the things of Our service'); practiced from 1502 when Ovando arrives; and the encomienda is formally legalized by the separate royal provision of 20 December 1503. Under the fiction of 'entrustment' (encomendar) for protection and Christianization, the crown grants a colonist tribute-or-labor over a set number of 'Indios' - a grant that, per Britannica, 'did not include a grant of land.' It is the FW-020 Iberian Reconquista instrument (tribute exacted from Muslims and Jews during the Reconquest) re-deployed via the Canaries to the Americas: the first formal extraction system for the New World and the antecedent the post-1550 repartimiento draft succeeds.

[E-W1-014-02] W1 1507 records TA MILESTONE
Waldseemuller 1507: the naming operation and record-erasure
Martin Waldseemuller's 1507 world map (Universalis Cosmographia), from the Gymnasium Vosagense at Saint-Die in Lorraine, is the first map to inscribe the name 'America', placed on the southern New World honoring Amerigo Vespucci, and the first to depict the Americas as a separate landmass distinct from Asia (LC holds the only surviving first-edition copy). This is representational-layer Software: it NAMES the Hardware, the cartographic counterpart to the jurisdictional instruments (Inter Caetera, Tordesillas) that partition the same space by legal title. Two things not to smooth: (a) Waldseemuller himself later dropped the name (1513/1516), so 'America' persisted by propagation and later-cartographer adoption, not by authorial decree, the first inscription that propagated, not a definitive naming; (b) it was a scholarly-cartographic act, NOT a sovereign or administrative act by any crown.
sources: [A live_verified] src-loc-waldseemuller-1507 https://www.loc.gov/item/2003626426/; [B citation_only] src-cosmographiae-introductio-1507
links: inter-caetera-1493, treaty-of-tordesillas-1494

Juan Lopez de Palacios Rubios drafts the Requerimiento for the Spanish Crown, promulgated 1513. [E-W1-016-02] W1 1518 legal TA MILESTONE
Asiento 1518: the transatlantic Staffing Agency contract begins
Charles V grants the first asiento (contract) to Lorenzo de Gorrevod on August 18, 1518. Contract authorizes the import of 4,000 African slaves into Spanish America over 8 years. Gorrevod immediately sublicenses to Genoese merchants (Centurion and De Grimaldis families) who further sublicense to Portuguese slavers.

[E-W1-017-01] W1 1521 military TA MILESTONE
Fall of Tenochtitlan: silver flow initialization
The fall of Tenochtitlan (13 August 1521) ends a ~93-day siege (22 May to 13 Aug 1521) waged not by Spaniards alone but by a coalition: Cortes's small Spanish force plus tens of thousands of indigenous allies, above all the Tlaxcalans (old rivals of the Aztecs), against a city already devastated by smallpox. The Aztec Triple Alliance is destroyed and New Spain is founded on its ruins. A violence-lane conquest (no population classified, no Software-Neutrality tag), it executes the FW-020 Reconquista template in the Americas - conquer, seize, redistribute, reclassify - and initializes the Mexican silver flow that will capitalize the Merchant-Banker network.

[E-W1-018-01] W1 1533 military TA
Cajamarca: the ransom-and-execute template
Francisco Pizarro, with about 180 men, enters Cajamarca on 15 Nov 1532 and the next day ambushes the Inca Atahualpa - after the friar Vicente de Valverde demands he accept the Christian faith and Charles V's sovereignty and Atahualpa refuses - capturing him and slaughtering thousands of unarmed retainers. Atahualpa is held for the famous ransom (a room of gold plus twice its volume in silver; some 24 tons), paid in full, and then executed 26 July 1533 (Hemming, The Conquest of the Incas, and modern scholarly consensus; Britannica's Atahuallpa biography gives 29 August). Per Britannica the ambush was 'prompted by the example of Hernan Cortes and Montezuma in Mexico' - the ransom-then-execute template. A violence-lane conquest executing the FW-020 template; no population classified (no Software-Neutrality tag).
sources: [A live_verified] src-xerez-verdadera-relacion-1534 https://www.biblioteca-antologica.org/es/wp-content/uploads/2018/05/XEREZ-Verdadera-relaci%C3%B3n-de-la-conquista-del-Per%C3%BA.pdf; [B live_verified] src-britannica-atahuallpa-cajamarca https://www.britannica.com/biography/Atahuallpa
links: cortes-tenochtitlan-1521, requerimiento-1513
threads: T-DISCOVERY-DOCTRINE

[E-W1-019-02] W1 1550 legal TA MILESTONE
Valladolid Debate 1550-51: the classification-question theater
Charles V convenes a junta at Valladolid (1550-1551) to judge whether the wars on the Indians are just. Sepulveda argues yes on Aristotelian natural-slavery grounds (the Indians as inferior 'homunculi'), plus punishing sins against nature, rescuing sacrifice victims, and clearing the way for preaching; he explicitly denies that mere disbelief is a just cause. Las Casas answers that the conquests are 'tiranicas, injustas y iniquas.' No indigenous participants are present. The junta reaches no binding verdict (one judge still unruled by 1557); Las Casas wins the argument but colonial extraction continues unchanged. The forensic value is this justification-vs-practice split, not a policy hinge.
sources: [A live_verified] src-sepulveda-democrates-secundus https://www.columbia.edu/acis/ets/CCREAD/sepulved.htm; [A live_verified] src-gilder-lehrman-valladolid-soto https://www.gilderlehrman.org/history-resources/spotlight-primary-source/bartolome-de-las-casas-debates-subjugation-indians-1550
links: requerimiento-1513, encomienda-1503, repartimiento-1550s, casta-system-1560s
threads: T-WHITENING-PROJECT

[E-W1-020-02] W1 1550s legal TA MILESTONE
Repartimiento: the rebrand-and-continue operation
REPARTIMIENTO here = the post-1549 rotational LABOR DRAFT (definite form c. 1575), disambiguated from two other referents of the word so they cannot be collapsed: (a) the early ~1492-1542 encomienda-adjacent grant, 'legally distinct but functionally identical' to encomienda, the ANTECEDENT; (b) the late-colonial repartimiento de mercancias, a forced goods-SALE, NOT a labor system, EXCLUDED here. The draft is a state-administered forced-labor quota: rotating levies to the mines (~5%) and seasonal agriculture (~10%), wages legally required and routinely ignored; it is the Peruvian mita / New Spain cuatequil. Coercive but distinct from chattel slavery, it persists in practice past the 1601/1609 reforms (Steele). Runtime tail: Dell (2010) finds the mita boundary predicts about 25% lower household consumption and about 6 points higher child stunting today.

[E-W1-021-01] W1 1555 legal TB
Muscovy Company 1555: joint-stock precursor to EIC/VOC
Mary I charters the Muscovy Company (Company of Merchant Adventurers to New Lands) in 1555. First English joint-stock company. Grants monopoly on trade with Russia via the White Sea route pioneered by Richard Chancellor (1553). Direct organizational precursor to the EIC (1600) and VOC (1602).
sources: [B live_verified] src-muscovy-company-1555 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muscovy_Company

[E-W1-021-02] W1 1555 financial TB
First formally chartered joint-stock company
1555: the Muscovy Company - the first major English chartered joint-stock company - pools investor capital for the Russia trade under a royal monopoly (the White Sea route pioneered by Richard Chancellor, 1553). The joint-stock financial form that the Levant Company and the East India Company inherit.
sources: [B live_verified] src-muscovy-company-1555 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muscovy_Company

[E-W1-022-01] W1 1545-1563 legal TA
Council of Trent: Counter-Reformation as Software patch
Council of Trent convenes December 13, 1545 and concludes December 4, 1563 in three phases. Counter-Reformation response to Protestant challenge (Luther 1517, Calvin 1536). Reaffirms papal authority, defines seven sacraments, standardizes Latin liturgy, produces the Index Librorum Prohibitorum (1559 under Paul IV).
sources: [A live_verified] src-tridentine-index-ten-rules-1564 https://bridwell.omeka.net/exhibits/show/luther/counterreformation/tenrules; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-index-prohibited-books https://www.worldhistory.org/article/2018/index-of-prohibited-books/
links: spanish-inquisition-1478

[E-W1-022-02] W1 1545-1563 information TA
Counter-Reformation doctrinal consolidation; Index of Forbidden Books 1559
The Council of Trent (1545-1563, 25 sessions), the Counter-Reformation's doctrinal response, decreed the Vulgate as sole authoritative scripture, defined justification, sacraments and penance, and condemned the Reformation as heresy. Its load-bearing output for this audit is the information-control instrument: the Index of Forbidden Books, whose purpose was 'preventing the laity from reading ... any work not expressly approved by the Church', and its Ten Rules (1564) - a systematic censorship apparatus (pre-1515 condemned books prohibited; all books by heresiarchs banned; vernacular Bibles requiring approval) that 'remained the core determinants for Catholic censorship until 1966.' It is the information-control face of the same Church enforcement machinery as the Inquisition. The framework reading (tier C, inference, not asserted from the Tridentine sources): this apparatus extends into a continuous narrative-control technology running to modern information control. SW-Neutrality NOT tagged: Trent classifies TEXT (heretical/unapproved), not populations.
sources: [A live_verified] src-tridentine-index-ten-rules-1564 https://bridwell.omeka.net/exhibits/show/luther/counterreformation/tenrules; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-index-prohibited-books https://www.worldhistory.org/article/2018/index-of-prohibited-books/
links: spanish-inquisition-1478

[E-W1-023-01] W1 1560s population TA MILESTONE
Casta system: Blood-purity scales to full racial taxonomy
From the 1560s and formalized across the 17th-18th centuries, New Spain's casta system scales the limpieza-de-sangre blood-purity doctrine (Toledo 1449) into a 16-plus category racial taxonomy, rendered in the casta-painting genre. Per Katzew (2004), the genre reads best as an elite view of how society should be arranged, a potent classification imaginary, not a window onto lived reality. It is a real ideological installation of race; the stronger claim that casta ran as a rigid operating bureaucracy fixing daily status is flagged down as framework projection the standard literature contests, reading enforcement as uneven. The apparatus is a doctrinal ancestor of Linnaean/Blumenbach race science and modern race-classification categories.
sources: [A citation_only] src-katzew-casta-painting-2004 https://archive.org/details/castapaintingima0000katz
links: franklin-observations-1751, toledo-blood-purity-1449
threads: T-WHITENING-PROJECT

[E-W1-024-01] W1 1565 land TB

sources: [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-manila-galleon https://www.worldhistory.org/Manila_Galleon/; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-manila-galleon https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manila_galleon
links: east-india-company-1600, voc-1602

[E-W1-024-02] W1 1565 financial TB
Manila Galleon 1565: silver-to-China conveyor belt
The Manila-Acapulco galleon trade begins in 1565, once Alonso de Arellano and Andres de Urdaneta discover the eastward return route (the tornaviaje) across the North Pacific - the breakthrough enabling a sustained crossing. For 250 years (1565-1815) the galleons carry Asian silk, spices and porcelain east to Acapulco and return loaded with American silver. It is the first sustained trans-Pacific trade route and the Pacific leg of the global silver-extraction system - how American silver reaches Asia - the Spanish crown-monopoly variant of the joint-stock/chartered-monopoly trade technology that the EIC and VOC develop into corporate form.
sources: [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-manila-galleon https://www.worldhistory.org/Manila_Galleon/; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-manila-galleon https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manila_galleon
links: east-india-company-1600, voc-1602

[E-W1-025-01] W1 1568 military TB
Dutch Revolt 1568: Amsterdam capitalization pipeline opens
The Seventeen Provinces of the Netherlands revolt against Philip II of Spain from 1568, opening the Eighty Years' War (1568-1648) that will produce the Dutch Republic. The triggers are heavy taxation and the persecution of Protestants; the revolt's naval arm, the Watergeuzen or 'Sea Beggars', seizes towns in Holland and Zeeland from 1572. The trigger-note this audit tracks: the long war fragments the Spanish/Habsburg financial position and begins the shift of financial gravity toward Amsterdam - the start (not the completion) of the Amsterdam-finance development that the VOC and the Sephardic-Amsterdam middleware later carry.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-eighty-years-war https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eighty_Years%27_War
links: spanish-armada-1588, voc-1602

[E-W1-026-01] W1 1577-80 military TA
Drake circumnavigation: state-privateer joint venture template
Francis Drake circumnavigates the globe in the Golden Hind between 1577 and 1580 - the first captain to survive the feat - raiding Spanish shipping and ports along the way and capturing treasure ships (in March 1579 the silver-laden Nuestra Senora de la Concepcion off Peru). The loot is perhaps 600,000 pounds, the whole annual revenue of England; the queen, who had secretly invested and instructed him to take Spanish treasure, receives 160,000 pounds and knights him in 1581. It is state-sanctioned privateering - the crown-licensed agent draining Spanish silver, the same private-enterprise-plus-crown-license pattern as Roanoke and the chartered companies.
sources: [A live_verified] src-drake-world-encompassed-1628 https://nationalhumanitiescenter.org/pds/amerbegin/contact/text5/drake.pdf; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-francis-drake https://www.worldhistory.org/Francis_Drake/
links: roanoke-1585, east-india-company-1600

[E-W1-027-01] W1 1580 legal TB
Iberian Union 1580-1640: peak Iberian consolidation as prelude to displacement
In 1580 Philip II of Spain succeeds to the Portuguese crown - after the death of King Sebastian at Alcacer Quibir (1578) and the short reign of Cardinal Henry - marching into Portugal and being recognized as king by the Cortes of Tomar in 1581. The Iberian Union (1580-1640) puts one monarch over both global colonial empires. The framework reading (tier C, inference, not asserted from the succession itself): the union consolidates the two colonial franchises under one crown, bringing the Tordesillas-divided halves of the non-Christian world under single management.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-iberian-union https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iberian_Union

[E-W1-028-01] W1 1581 legal TB
Levant Company 1581: joint-stock corporate form scaled
Elizabeth I charters the Turkey Company in 1581 for English trade with the Ottoman Empire; in 1592 it merges with the Venice Company (1583) to form the Levant Company, a chartered joint-stock monopoly on Levantine trade. Levant Company merchants finance the 1591 Lancaster voyage that directly precedes the East India Company (1600): the corporate-form template scales Muscovy (1555) to Turkey/Levant (1581/1592) to the EIC (1600). Direct organizational ancestor.
sources: [B live_verified] src-levant-company-1581 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Levant_Company
links: muscovy-company-1555-joint-stock-precursor-1555, east-india-company-1600

[E-W1-028-02] W1 1581 financial TB
Second English joint-stock; Mediterranean trade with Ottoman Empire
1581/1592: the Turkey Company (1581) and the Venice Company (1583) merge into the Levant Company (1592), a chartered joint-stock monopoly on Ottoman trade. Its merchants finance the 1591 Lancaster voyage that directly precedes the East India Company (1600) - the Muscovy to Levant to EIC joint-stock lineage.
sources: [B live_verified] src-levant-company-1581 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Levant_Company

[E-W1-029-01] W1 1585 military TB
Roanoke 1585-1590: English challenge to Iberian monopoly begins on the ground
Elizabeth I grants Sir Walter Raleigh a patent in 1584 to settle 'remote, heathen and barbarous lands ... not actually possessed of any Christian Prince' (the terra-nullius/heathen-land framing). The first colony (1585, Ralph Lane) fails and is evacuated by Drake in 1586; the second (1587, John White) - the 'Lost Colony', birthplace of Virginia Dare - is found deserted in 1590 ('Croatoan' carved), its relief delayed by the 1588 Armada. Roanoke was a privateering base as much as a settlement, 'ideally suited ... to prey upon Spanish treasure ships', financed by combining colonization with privateering - the private-enterprise-plus-crown-license model of the Capitulations and the chartered companies, and Discovery-Doctrine logic applied to English settlement.

[E-W1-030-01] W1 1588 military TB
Spanish Armada 1588: naval displacement begins
In 1588 Philip II sends the Spanish Armada - about 132 ships and some 24,000 men - to invade England, depose Elizabeth I, and reverse the English Reformation, counting on English Catholics. The faster English fleet defeats it at the Battle of Gravelines, and continuing storms scatter it around Scotland and Ireland; only half returns to Spain. The Dutch blockade of Parma's army in the Netherlands is a decisive factor. The Armada's failure is the strategic turning point that checks Spanish power and opens the way for English Atlantic expansion - including the colonization it delays the same year at Roanoke.
sources: [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-spanish-armada https://www.worldhistory.org/Spanish_Armada/
links: roanoke-1585, dutch-revolt-1568

[E-W1-031-01] W1 1594 military TB
Nine Years War 1594-1603: Iberian classification template redeployed on Ireland
Hugh O'Neill (Earl of Tyrone) and Hugh Roe O'Donnell lead the Nine Years' War (1594-1603), primarily in Ulster, against the Elizabethan conquest of Ireland. Spanish aid lands at Kinsale in 1601 but the war is decided for the English there; defeat leads to the Flight of the Earls (1607), the confiscation of the earls' lands (1608), and the Plantation of Ulster. The tier-calibrated framework note: this runs the conquest-confiscation-settler-plantation template on a European Christian (Irish Catholic) population - the Software-Neutrality principle (the code runs on Europeans) - but the mechanism is Anglo-plantation, distinct from the Iberian blood-classification line. The war is the trigger; the taggable classification node is the Plantation of Ulster (1609), not yet built.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-nine-years-war-ireland https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nine_Years%27_War_(Ireland)

[E-W1-032-01] W1 1600 legal TA MILESTONE
East India Company 1600: joint-stock corporate form fully installed
Elizabeth I grants a royal charter on 31 December 1600 to the Governor and Company of Merchants of London Trading into the East Indies - a limited joint-stock company with a monopoly (initially 15 years) on all English trade east of the Cape of Good Hope. The charter delegates sovereign powers: the Company is 'permitted to wage war' and to 'exercise sovereignty in the name of the English Crown', arming ships, appointing governors, and making war or alliances. It is the corporate-as-quasi-state instrument - a private mercantilist corporation exercising sovereign functions under state charter, the corporate-grid technology in developed form and twin to the VOC (1602).
sources: [A live_verified] src-eic-first-charter-1600 https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Page:A_Collection_of_Charters_and_Statutes_relating_to_the_East_India_Company.pdf/9; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-east-india-company https://www.worldhistory.org/East_India_Company/
links: voc-1602, manila-galleon-1565

[E-W1-033-01] W1 1602 legal TB MILESTONE
VOC 1602: hinge to W2 Corporate Grid; Amsterdam Tier 1 institutionalized
The Staten-Generaal charters the Vereenigde Oostindische Compagnie (VOC) on 20 March 1602 - a 21-year charter, 6.4 million guilders in capital, an exclusive monopoly on Dutch trade in Asia. It is the world's first publicly traded company (open to public share issues) and, though chartered for trade, 'became a colonial power ... with the right to make treaties, build fortifications, and conduct military operations.' The VOC is the fully-developed corporate-grid form - a chartered joint-stock monopoly wielding sovereign functions - the twin developed corporation to the EIC (1600); together they are the corporate technology the Manila galleon's crown-monopoly variant precedes.
sources: [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-voc https://www.worldhistory.org/Dutch_East_India_Company/
links: east-india-company-1600, manila-galleon-1565

[E-W1-033-02] W1 1602 financial TB
First company to issue publicly tradable shares; first sovereign-power grant to corporate entity
framework: W1-to-W2 hinge event; joint-stock corporate form scaled to sovereign power; Amsterdam-Sephardic Tier 1 middleware institutionalized; direct doctrinal ancestor of every subsequent multinational corporation including modern BlackRock-Vanguard-State Street
assessment: Weight: Proven | Warrant: Closed

[E-W1-034-01] W1 1550 population TC
The Moor/native juridical seam: invincible ignorance vs culpable rejection (audit inference)
The audit's forensic inference, not a quotation. The Salamanca scholastic just-war tradition holds two juridical categories with different war-standing: the invincibly ignorant, who never met the Gospel (the Amerindians), and the culpable rejecter, who knew and refused Christ (the Moors). Valladolid works inside this tradition without stating the line, and Sepulveda's Democrates Secundus explicitly excludes mere disbelief as a just cause; the Requerimiento asserts only blanket papal jurisdiction. The distinction is drawn in no reached primary. The audit synthesizes it from the tradition (Vitoria, De Indis; Pagden, The Fall of Natural Man), held with confidence as tier-C inference, not primary text. It is the category the managers' own theology drew, the same argument as the far-timeline Moor-to-Negro reclassification overwrite.
sources: [A citation_only] src-vitoria-de-indis
links: 1924-triple-lock-reclassification-chain-step-1924
threads: T-SOFTWARE-NEUTRALITY

[E-W2-001-01] W2 1600 financial TB
East India Company chartered
English EIC: Crown-licensed monopoly on Asian trade, chartered by Elizabeth I on 31 December 1600. Thomas Smythe is already governor of the Muscovy and Levant companies when he becomes the EIC's first governor, a post he holds to 1621 apart from a break in 1605-07. He obtains the Virginia Company charter in 1609 and serves as its treasurer until 1618. For two decades the EIC's general assemblies meet in his own house at Philpot Lane.
sources: [B live_verified] src-encyc-va-smythe https://encyclopediavirginia.org/entries/smythe-sir-thomas-ca-1558-1625/; [B citation_only] src-britannica-smythe https://www.britannica.com/money/Thomas-Smythe; [B citation_only] src-hop-smythe-1558-1625 https://www.historyofparliamentonline.org/volume/1604-1629/member/smythe-sir-thomas-1558-1625
links: east-india-company-1600, levant-company-1581-joint-stock-corporate-1581, voc-1602

[E-W2-001-02] W2 1600b catastrophe TB
Huaynaputina eruption → global famine
The largest recorded eruption in South American history, VEI 6, from 19 February 1600. Its sulphur aerosols cooled the Northern Hemisphere to its coldest summer in six centuries the next year, wrecked harvests across Europe, Asia and the Americas, and helped trigger the Russian famine of 1601-1603. A global exogenous shock at the window’s opening, external to the American Levant and to the Siphon.
sources: [A citation_only] src-desilva-zielinski-huaynaputina; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-huaynaputina https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huaynaputina
links: port-royal-earthquake-1692, lisbon-earthquake-1755

[E-W2-002-01] W2 1606 financial TA
Virginia Company chartered
King James I, April 10. Private corporation granted permanent military authority to "expulse and resist" anyone without license. All land held "in free and common soccage" from the Manor at East-Greenwich - the colonists are TENANTS, never owners. Nemo dat: tenants cannot grant allodial title downstream.

[E-W2-003-01] W2 1607 violence TB
Jamestown established
First permanent English settlement in the American Levant. Built on Powhatan Confederacy territory. "Starving Time" (1609-10): 80% mortality. The franchise nearly fails. Will be restocked with indentured labor and then classified labor.

Bank of Amsterdam (Wisselbank)

[E-W2-004-02] W2 1609b land TB
Henry Hudson claims Hudson River
Henry Hudson, sailing for the Dutch East India Company aboard the Halve Maen, enters the river that now bears his name in the summer of 1609, reaching near present-day Albany before the shallows turn him back. He finds no passage to Asia, but his survey of the coast founds the Dutch claim to the region that becomes New Netherland.

[E-W2-005-01] W2 1619 population TA
First Africans at Jamestown
“20. and odd Negroes” taken from the São João Bautista, a Portuguese slaver out of São Paulo de Luanda bound for Vera Cruz with 350 captives, 200 of them licensed under asiento to Seville investors. Privateers White Lion and Treasurer, both out of Flushing under foreign letters of marque, took about 60 off Campeche. White Lion reached Point Comfort late August 1619; Governor Yeardley and Cape Merchant Abraham Piersey bought them for victuals.

[E-W2-005-03] W2 1619b innovation TB
Virginia tobacco economy established
John Rolfe’s sweeter Orinoco strain, first cultivated around 1611 and exported from 1612, makes tobacco Virginia’s first profitable export: 20,000 pounds shipped in 1617, 40,000 in 1618. The cash crop drives the colony’s hunger for labour, indentured then enslaved. The row is filed at 1619 with the first Africans, though the economy it names was established several years earlier.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-john-rolfe https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Rolfe

[E-W2-006-01] W2 1621 financial TB
Dutch West India Company (WIC) chartered
Chartered 3 June 1621; founder Joannes de Laet; five chambers (Amsterdam, Hoorn, Rotterdam, Groningen, Middelburg) under the Heeren XIX. Held the Atlantic monopoly on trade and warfare 1624-1674, reinstated 1674-1738, the entity surviving to 1791. Shipped at least 85,000 enslaved people before 1674 and at least 180,000 after, both floors, so at least 265,000 in total, about a quarter of all Dutch slave trade. Supplied Spanish colonies via Curaçao as entrepôt, an indirect stake in the Asiento.
sources: [A citation_only] src-postma-dutch-atlantic-slave-trade; [A citation_only] src-den-heijer-geschiedenis-wic; [B live_verified] src-low-countries-wic-slave-trade https://www.the-low-countries.com/article/approved-by-the-bible-the-slave-trade-of-the-dutch-west-india-company/; [B live_verified] src-project-manifest-wic https://www.projectmanifest.eu/the-wic-the-dutch-west-india-company-en-fr/
links: voc-1602, asiento-first-1518, piet-hein-captures-spanish-silver-fleet-1628

Virginia Company charter revoked. Colony transferred from private corporation to direct Crown control. The franchise failed as a company; the Crown takes over directly. Same extraction, new management letterhead.

[E-W2-007-02] W2 1624b land TB
Dutch establish Fort Orange (Albany)
In 1624 the Dutch West India Company builds Fort Orange on the Hudson at present-day Albany - the first permanent Dutch settlement in New Netherland, replacing the abandoned Fort Nassau. It is built on Mahican land without consent (unlike later purchase-based settlement) and operates as a fur-trade post at the Iroquois/Algonquian crossroads: the WIC's land-claim-and-extraction node in North America.
sources: [B live_verified] src-fort-orange-1624 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fort_Orange_(New_Netherland)

[E-W2-008-01] W2 1628 financial TB
Piet Hein captures Spanish Silver Fleet
WIC admiral seizes entire Spanish treasure fleet off Cuba. ~11.5 million guilders - enough to finance the conquest of Brazil. Spanish Potosí silver redirected to Dutch accounts. The Staffing Agencies pirate each other’s revenue streams.
sources: [B live_verified] src-piet-hein-1628 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piet_Pieterszoon_Hein

[E-W2-009-01] W2 1636b financial TB
Tulip Mania
Dutch tulip futures peak in late 1636 and collapse in February 1637. The image of nationwide ruin is folklore: Goldgar’s archival work finds the trade confined to a fairly small circle of wealthy merchants and craftsmen, fewer than half a dozen in real financial trouble, and, money having largely not yet changed hands, the crash cost almost no one anything. The shock was to a network of values, not to the economy.
sources: [A citation_only] src-goldgar-tulipmania; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-tulip-mania https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tulip_mania
links: bank-of-amsterdam-wisselbank-1609, first-government-bonds-issued-1693, bubble-act-1720
threads: T-MERCHANT-BANKER-CONSTANT

[E-W2-009-02] W2 1636 violence TB
Pequot War
The Pequot War (1636-1638): at the Mystic Massacre (26 May 1637) English colonists with Mohegan and Narragansett allies burn the Pequot fort, killing over 700, mostly women and children. Modern scholarship holds the war's causes were a pretext for English expansion - a land-clearing operation. Survivors are sold into slavery in the West Indies and Bermuda or given as slaves to the allied tribes: the war is the trigger, a Track-C-style labour export, not itself the classification instrument (that is the Treaty of Hartford).
sources: [B live_verified] src-pequot-war-hartford https://www.worldhistory.org/Pequot_War/
links: 1924-triple-lock-reclassification-chain-step-1924, nine-years-war-ireland-1594
threads: T-THREE-TRACK-LABOR

[E-W2-009-03] W2 1636 population TB
Pequot survivors enslaved & exported
The Treaty of Hartford (21 September 1638) legally abolishes the Pequot as a people: by the treaty's own words the Pequots 'shall no more be called Pequots but Narragansetts and Mohegans', and the victors 'shall not suffer them for to live in the country that was formerly theirs but now is the English by conquest' (taking their enslavers' names, barred from their land). It is a reclassification-by-legal-erasure operation - abolishing a specific polity's Noun by treaty (Software-Neutrality, tagged on the name-abolition; now resting on the treaty primary). It is the direct structural ancestor of Plecker's 1924 abolition of 'Moor' from Virginia records: the same legal-name-erasure ~286 years apart, the Noun-to-Adjective spine.
sources: [B live_verified] src-pequot-war-hartford https://www.worldhistory.org/Pequot_War/; [B live_verified] src-treaty-hartford-1638 https://venturesmithcolonialct.org/library/treaty-of-hartford-1638/
links: 1924-triple-lock-reclassification-chain-step-1924, nine-years-war-ireland-1594
threads: T-THREE-TRACK-LABOR, T-SOFTWARE-NEUTRALITY, T-NOUN-TO-ADJECTIVE

[E-W2-009-04] W2 1636 land TB
Pequot territory seized
By the Treaty of Hartford the Pequot territory is divided among the victors (the Mohegan, the Narragansett, and the colonists), and survivors are forbidden to return to their ancestral lands. Land-clearing completed: the war removes the population, the treaty extinguishes the claim.
sources: [B live_verified] src-pequot-war-hartford https://www.worldhistory.org/Pequot_War/; [B live_verified] src-treaty-hartford-1638 https://venturesmithcolonialct.org/library/treaty-of-hartford-1638/

[E-W2-010-01] W2 1640 population TB
John Punch case
Virginia. Three servants escape: two European, one African. Europeans receive extended indentures. The African (John Punch) receives lifetime servitude. First documented case of racial differential sentencing in English colonies. The classification tested as criminal punishment.

[E-W2-010-03] W2 1640b innovation TB
Sugar revolution begins, Barbados
Sugar cane arrives from Dutch Brazil around 1640 and remakes Barbados. Within two years it is the focus of the economy; small tobacco holdings are consolidated into large plantations, and the labour demand shifts decisively to enslaved Africans, soon three for every planter. The sugar revolution is the economic pressure behind the island’s slave codes: the 1661 Act that first comprehensively governs the enslaved is written to control the workforce sugar requires.

[E-W2-011-02] W2 1655 legal TA
Johnson v. Casor - “Negro” = Property
Northampton County Court. First civil case declaring a person property WITHOUT A CRIME. Anthony Johnson (free Black landholder) uses the "Negro" classification to claim Casor as property. The Asiento’s inventory classification now functional in English courts. The Noun (Free Landholder) deploys the Adjective (Negro) - and the Adjective will later consume the Noun.

[E-W2-012-01] W2 ~1655 counter-move TB MILESTONE
★ Maroon communities forming - Counter-Move
Across the Caribbean and the American interior, people who escape the classification form independent, self-governing communities. In Jamaica, when the Spanish flee the English conquest of 1655, the people they held take their own freedom and coalesce in the interior as the Maroons (from the Spanish cimarrón, wild, untamed). They hold their ground by force until the treaties of 1739 and 1740 acknowledge their autonomy and land, in exchange for military service and the return of later runaways.
sources: [A citation_only] src-campbell-maroons-jamaica; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-jamaican-maroons https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamaican_Maroons
links: cromwell-seizes-jamaica-1655, fort-mose-1738
threads: T-COLLAPSE-SURVIVAL-N1

[E-W2-013-01] W2 1656 legal TA
Elizabeth Key lawsuit - THE EXIT DOOR THAT TRIGGERS PARTUS
Daughter of English planter Thomas Key and an enslaved African woman. When John Mottrom's estate is settled in 1655 she is inventoried as “Elizabeth the Negro woman & her sonne”. She sues on three grounds: paternal status at common law, Christian baptism, and an expired nine-year indenture. A Northumberland jury frees her 20 January 1656; the Council reverses 12 March; the Assembly finds “the Child of a Woman slave begott by a free-man ought to bee free”. She wins 21 July 1656.
sources: [A live_verified] src-dvb-elizabeth-key https://old.lva.virginia.gov/public/dvb/bio.asp?b=Key_Elizabeth_fl_1655-1660; [B citation_only] src-blackpast-elizabeth-key https://blackpast.org/african-american-history/grinstead-elizabeth-key-1630/; [B citation_only] src-nyhs-wams-elizabeth-key https://wams.nyhistory.org/life-story/elizabeth-key-grinstead/
links: john-punch-case-1640, partus-sequitur-ventrem-1662, virginia-baptism-not-freedom-1667
threads: T-WHITENING-PROJECT

[E-W2-014-01] W2 1660 financial TA
Company of Royal Adventurers
1660: Charles II grants the Company of Royal Adventurers trading into Africa its original charter, re-incorporated 10 January 1663. Governor: James, Duke of York, the King's brother. The Crown enters the African trade at the ownership level, a generation before the RAC. After heavy wartime losses the company surrenders its charter, and the Royal African Company supersedes it in 1672.
sources: [A live_verified] src-csp-colonial-awi-vol7-rac-charter https://www.british-history.ac.uk/cal-state-papers/colonial/america-west-indies/vol7/pp404-417; [A citation_only] src-davies-royal-african-company
links: asiento-first-1518, royal-african-company-chartered-1672, rac-monopoly-broken-slave-trade-deregulated-1698
threads: T-ASIENTO

[E-W2-014-02] W2 1660b legal TA
Navigation Acts
Navigation Act, 13 September 1660. Enumerated colonial goods, sugar, tobacco, cotton, indigo, ginger and dyewoods, may ship only to England or English colonies, in English-built ships crewed three-quarters English. The mercantile frame that funnels colonial trade through England: colonial wealth routed to English shippers and merchants, colonial economies subordinated to the imperial account. Basis of nearly two centuries of trade regulation.
sources: [A live_verified] src-navigation-act-1660-statute https://www.british-history.ac.uk/statutes-realm/vol5/pp246-250; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-navigation-acts https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Navigation_Acts
links: east-india-company-chartered-1600, company-of-royal-adventurers-1660, rac-monopoly-broken-slave-trade-deregulated-1698
threads: T-FRANCHISE-TRANSFER

[E-W2-015-01] W2 1661 legal TA
Barbados Slave Code
September 1661, twenty-three clauses: “An Act for the better ordering and governing of Negroes,” the first comprehensive slave code in the English colonies. Its preamble puts the enslaved outside common law as a “heathenish, brutish, and uncertain dangerous kind of people,” to be protected as “other goods and chattels.” The same assembly passed a parallel Christian Servants code the same day. The template travels: Jamaica 1664, revised 1684, South Carolina 1691.
sources: [A citation_only] src-barbados-negroes-act-1661-pro; [A live_verified] src-unb-slavery-freedom-barbados-1661 https://slaveryandfreedomlaws.lib.unb.ca/laws/barbados-1661; [A citation_only] src-rugemer-mastery-race-slave-codes; [B citation_only] src-engerman-drescher-paquette-slavery
links: barbados-sugar-revolution-1640, code-noir-1685, virginia-slave-codes-1705, sc-negro-act-1740
threads: T-THREE-TRACK-LABOR

[E-W2-016-01] W2 1662 population TA
Partus Sequitur Ventrem - Biological Lock
Virginia colonial statute, Act XII of December 1662. “All children borne in this country shalbe held bond or free only according to the condition of the mother”, reversing the common-law presumption that status descended from the father. The classification tested in Johnson v. Casor (1655) becomes hereditary, passing by birth rather than by contract, crime, or conversion. Follows the Elizabeth Key suit of 1656, which tested the paternity rule the act abolishes.
sources: [A live_verified] src-va-act-xii-1662-partus https://encyclopediavirginia.org/entries/negro-womens-children-to-serve-according-to-the-condition-of-the-mother-1662/; [A live_verified] src-dvb-elizabeth-key https://old.lva.virginia.gov/public/dvb/bio.asp?b=Key_Elizabeth_fl_1655-1660; [C citation_only] src-corporate-caselaw-layer-1602-1713
links: elizabeth-key-lawsuit-the-exit-door-1656, casta-system-1560s, virginia-code-1682, virginia-slave-codes-1705
threads: T-WHITENING-PROJECT

[E-W2-016-03] W2 1662b information TA
Schott Physica Curiosa - JESUIT KNOWLEDGE INVERSION
Gaspar Schott, SJ, Physica Curiosa (Würzburg, 1662): twelve books, the first six on the miraculous, a section among them on human and animal monsters, the last six on natural marvels, densely illustrated with dozens of engraved plates. Schott is a compiler, not an originator, aggregating the existing European literature and illustration of human variation from Gesner and Aldrovandi. It appears the same decade as Partus, Barbados 1661 and the Virginia codes.
sources: [A live_verified] src-schott-physica-curiosa-1662 https://archive.org/details/chepfl-lipr-AXB32_01; [B live_verified] src-bhl-schott-monsters https://blog.biodiversitylibrary.org/2013/05/monsters-scientific-revolution-and.html; [B citation_only] src-salomao-schott-physica-curiosa-2009

[E-W2-017-02] W2 1663 legal TB
New France becomes royal colony
18 September 1663. Louis XIV dissolves the Company of One Hundred Associates and takes New France into the royal domain, then charters the French West India Company in 1664 to run its commerce. France now builds a colonial grid parallel to England's: Canada for fur, the Caribbean for sugar, later Louisiana. That standing French infrastructure is what makes the Bourbon Mortgage of 1778 intelligible.

[E-W2-018-02] W2 1664 land TB
New Netherland → New York
27 August 1664. Four English frigates take New Amsterdam; the whole Dutch seaboard colony, Manhattan, the Hudson Valley, the future Wall Street, becomes New York. The Articles of Capitulation preserve Dutch property and trade, so the commercial machinery carries on under English management. Confirmed at the Treaty of Breda in 1667.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-new-netherland https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Netherland

[E-W2-019-01] W2 1665 catastrophe TB
Great Plague of London
1665-1666. Roughly 100,000 die, nearly a quarter of London, in eighteen months. The court and the wealthy flee, the King to Salisbury then Oxford, while the poor are held back by health certificates and refused entry to the villages beyond. A mortality-and-displacement catastrophe, the first of the two London shocks; its long consequence is the rebuilding that follows the next year's fire.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-great-plague-london https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Plague_of_London
links: black-death-1347-51, great-fire-of-london-1666

[E-W2-020-03] W2 1666 legal TA
Cestui Que Vie Act
Statute 18 Cha. 2 c. 11. Where a land tenure ran on the life of a named person (cestui que vie) who had gone overseas, those in remainder could not prove the life had ended and were kept out of the estate. The Act sets a rule of evidence: after seven years' absence with no proof of life, the person is presumed dead and the estate settles. It resolves title where a life cannot be accounted for. Passed the year London's records burned.
sources: [A live_verified] src-cestui-que-vie-act-1666 https://www.legislation.gov.uk/aep/Cha2/18-19/11/contents
links: great-plague-of-london-1665

[E-W2-021-01] W2 1670 financial TB
Hudson’s Bay Company chartered
2 May 1670. Charles II charters the Governor and Company of Adventurers of England trading into Hudson's Bay, with Prince Rupert as first governor. It receives Rupert's Land, the whole Hudson Bay watershed, roughly 1.5 million square miles, over a third of modern Canada, and a monopoly on its fur trade. The charter model, Levant to EIC to Virginia to RAC, now closes the grid's northern quarter.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-hudsons-bay-company https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hudson%27s_Bay_Company
