[E-W2-022-01] W2 1670b legal TB
Anthony Johnson estate seized
August 1670 escheat inquisition, John Stringer escheator for Northampton and Accomack: FIFTY ACRES IN ACCOMACK COUNTY, already in the possession of Johnson's son Richard, escheated on the finding that Anthony Johnson, lately deceased, 'was a negro and by consequence an alien and for that cause the said land doth escheat'. The parcel passed to a white neighbour, George Parker. (Anthony's earlier 250-acre Northampton headright is a separate holding and is not what this inquisition took.) THE ADJECTIVE CONSUMES ITS CREATOR. Johnson used “Negro” to win Casor in 1655; now the same classification destroys his family. His sons John and Richard flee to Delaware and RENT land they once would have inherited. Complete arc: Antonio the Moor arrives as Noun → wins as Noun → deploys the Adjective → Adjective consumes the Noun → descendants reduced from landowners to tenants.

[E-W2-023-01] W2 1672 financial TA
Royal African Company chartered
Chartered 27 September 1672 by Charles II, superseding the surrendered 1663 Company of Royal Adventurers; Governor James, Duke of York, James II from 1685. Ships roughly 5,000 enslaved people a year through the 1680s, mainly to the Caribbean and Virginia: 90,000-100,000 in the monopoly years 1672-1689, about twenty-three voyages annually. Across 1672-1731 it invested in voyages carrying 186,748 people, 16,077 of whom died at sea. Captives branded DY for the Duke, or RAC on the chest.
sources: [A citation_only] src-pettigrew-freedoms-debt; [A citation_only] src-davies-royal-african-company; [A live_verified] src-csp-colonial-awi-vol7-rac-charter https://www.british-history.ac.uk/cal-state-papers/colonial/america-west-indies/vol7/pp404-417; [B citation_only] src-smithsonian-rac-stock-certificate https://transcription.si.edu/project/29682

[E-W2-024-01] W2 1675 violence TB
King Philip’s War
1675-1678. Metacom (King Philip) leads a Wampanoag and Narragansett alliance against the New England colonies. One of the bloodiest wars per capita in American history: of roughly ninety towns more than half are attacked and twelve destroyed outright, and about one-tenth of the colonies' military-age men are killed. Indigenous power in southern New England is broken; surrendering Indians and the women and children of Mount Hope are sold into slavery.
sources: [A citation_only] src-lepore-name-of-war; [B citation_only] src-britannica-king-philips-war https://www.britannica.com/event/King-Philips-War; [B live_verified] src-colonialwarsct-1675 https://www.colonialwarsct.org/1675.htm

threads: T-THREE-TRACK-LABOR

Bacon’s Rebellion
Multiracial uprising of indentured servants and enslaved people against Virginia elite. White and Black laborers unite against the planter class. The Managers’ response: DIVIDE the labor pool by RACE. The 1682 and 1705 racial codes are the direct legislative response to this moment.

[E-W2-026-01] W2 1680 counter-move TB MILESTONE
★ Pueblo Revolt - Most successful indigenous counter-move
August 10, 1680. Popé of San Juan, a Tewa medicine man, leads an almost universal Pueblo uprising. Governor Otermín and the Spanish flee Santa Fe by 21 August. About 400 die, 21 of the province's 33 Franciscan friars among them, roughly a quarter of New Mexico's Spanish population. Some 2,000 survivors retreat to El Paso. A Spanish force of 300 is repelled in 1681: the twelve years are defended, not merely uncontested, until Vargas reconquers in 1692.
sources: [A citation_only] src-knaut-pueblo-revolt-1680; [B citation_only] src-britannica-pueblo-rebellion https://www.britannica.com/event/Pueblo-Rebellion; [B live_verified] src-penn-expedition-pueblo-revolt https://www.penn.museum/sites/expedition/researching-the-pueblo-revolt-of-1680/
links: requerimiento-1513, encomienda-1503, spanish-royal-decree-florida-sanctuary-policy-1693
threads: T-COLLAPSE-SURVIVAL-N1

[E-W2-027-01] W2 1682 population TA
"Negroes, Moors, Mollattoes" merged
Virginia merges “Negroes, Moors, Mollattoes or Indians” whose “parentage and native country are not christian” into one enslaveable category, later conversion no bar. It carves out one exception: “all servants except Turkes and Moores, whilest in amity with his majesty.” The exemption turns on diplomatic amity with the Crown, not Christian status: subjects of Muslim states at peace with England are set apart where other non-Christians are not.

James II becomes King
6 February 1685. The Royal African Company's governor since 1660 becomes King of England, and keeps the post. James, Duke of York, whose initials DY are branded on the chests of the enslaved the RAC ships, some 5,000 a year in the 1680s, now holds the throne. Crown, chartered slaver and classification meet in one man, until the Glorious Revolution removes both king and governorship.

[E-W2-028-02] W2 1685b legal TA
Code Noir - France
Royal ordinance of Louis XIV, 30 March 1685, drafted by Colbert, 60 articles. Defines the enslaved as movable, inheritable property (Art. XLIV), barred from owning goods or testifying against free persons. One ordinance sorts three populations by legal category: Article I expels all Jews as “declared enemies of the Christian name,” Articles III and V suppress Protestant worship, Article II mandates Catholic baptism for every slave. In force, with modifications, over 160 years.
sources: [A live_verified] src-code-noir-1685-garrigus https://louisiana-anthology.org/texts/louis_xiv/louis_xiv--code_noir_english.html; [A citation_only] src-nps-code-noir-transcription; [B citation_only] src-lef-code-noir https://revolution.chnm.org/d/335/; [A citation_only] src-niort-richard-code-noir-comparees

[E-W2-029-04] W2 1688d information TB
De la Vega: Confusión de confusiones
Joseph (Penso) de la Vega, a Sephardic Jew of Amsterdam, publishes Confusion de confusiones in 1688 - the first study written about a stock exchange, the Amsterdam exchange (whose principal stock was the VOC), describing options (puts and calls), futures, margin buying and bull-and-bear conspiracies. Printed at a clandestine press to evade Sephardic rabbinical censorship, it sits at the intersection of the Sephardic-Amsterdam finance middleware and the information/censorship lane.

[E-W2-030-02] W2 1688c information TB
Germantown Quaker Petition
18 February 1688. Four Quakers at Germantown, Pennsylvania, led by Francis Daniel Pastorius, sign the first public document in the English colonies to protest slavery. On the Golden Rule they ask how Christians can hold others in a bondage they would refuse for themselves. The meeting neither adopts nor rejects it, and it disappears into the archives for 150 years.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-germantown-petition https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1688_Germantown_Quaker_Petition_Against_Slavery

[E-W2-031-01] W2 1688b financial TB
Lloyd’s Coffee House - slave voyage insurance
Edward Lloyd’s coffeehouse, Tower Street, London. Becomes the headquarters of maritime insurance; by the 1690s slave voyages are routinely underwritten here. Insurance converts classified people into ACTUARIAL PRODUCTS: each voyage a priced portfolio of shipwreck, mutiny, disease and mortality risk. VENETIAN PRE-HISTORY: the practice does NOT originate here; Venetian and Genoese merchants developed the instruments in the Mediterranean before Lloyd’s existed (W0).

1693. The mechanism that will bind the taxed population to the instruments held above them is now in place.

[E-W2-037-01] W2 1696 financial TB
Great Recoinage - First Silver Demotion
Half the silver coin is clipped or forged, its bullion worth more abroad than its face at home. All silver is called in, melted and reissued; Isaac Newton, Warden of the Mint, runs the operation, and over five million pounds is struck by 1700. The recoinage is no financial success, and England drifts toward the gold standard. In the framework’s reading, silver, the people’s metal, is demoted as gold, the Managers’, is elevated.
sources: [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-great-recoinage-1696 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Recoinage_of_1696

[E-W2-038-01] W2 1698 financial TB
RAC monopoly broken - slave trade deregulated
1698. Parliament opens the African trade to private merchants on a ten-per-cent duty, ending the RAC's monopoly; the separate traders quickly carry the greater share, several times the Company's volume within a decade (Pettigrew). The extraction function disperses from one chartered company to a whole market, and decentralising it raises its throughput rather than lowering it, the N1 pattern.

[E-W2-039-01] W2 1701 violence TB
War of the Spanish Succession begins
1701-1714. The death of Charles II of Spain without an heir opens a war over the Spanish succession and the European balance of power, Britain, Austria and the Netherlands against France and Spain. Control of the Spanish empire's commerce is one of the stakes, and the Asiento, the contract to supply enslaved people to Spanish America, passes to Britain as one outcome at the Treaty of Utrecht in 1713.

[E-W2-042-01] W2 1667 legal TA
Virginia law: baptism does not confer freedom
Virginia General Assembly, September 1667. “The conferring of baptisme doth not alter the condition of the person as to his bondage or ffreedome.” Baptism no longer frees the enslaved, so masters may “endeavour the propagation of christianity” without risking their property. The religious exit that Dum Diversas left implied, conversion out of subjugation, is closed by statute. Confirmed and folded into the comprehensive code of 1705.
sources: [A live_verified] src-va-act-1667-baptism https://encyclopediavirginia.org/primary-documents/an-act-declaring-that-baptisme-of-slaves-doth-not-exempt-them-from-bondage-1667/

[E-W2-043-01] W2 1657 legal TB
Scarborough forges debt letter - Johnson land seized
Northampton County. Edmund Scarborough, a white planter and local power broker, produces a debt letter in Anthony Johnson’s name. Johnson was illiterate and could not have written it. The court nonetheless awards Scarborough 100 acres of Johnson’s land. The same court that had enforced Johnson’s property claim over Casor two years earlier now uses the “Negro” classification to strip Johnson of his own estate.

[E-W2-044-01] W2 1705 legal TB MILESTONE
Virginia Slave Codes consolidated
Virginia General Assembly consolidates four decades of piecemeal statute into a single slave code. “Negro” and “non-Christian” are merged into one permanent, heritable status, and baptism is confirmed as no longer an exit. The classification chain that runs from John Punch (1640) through Johnson v. Casor (1655), Partus (1662) and the 1682 merger reaches its codified form: servitude by birth, sealed against crime, contract and conversion alike.

HEIR FIREWALL , 1787 Treaty (Five Reasons for Constitutional Inclusion)
CROSS-REF: Every military operation in this window must operate AROUND this instrument. The 1787 Treaty of Peace and Friendship Supreme Law under Art. VI is the firewall. (A5.5) WHY THE TREATY WAS INCLUDED IN THE CONSTITUTION five converging reasons: (1) DIPLOMATIC NECESSITY Morocco was the first nation to recognize American independence (1777); the new Republic needed international legitimacy and could not afford to alienate its first ally. (2) MILITARY REALITY The Sultan had defeated France at Larache (1765), expelled Portugal from Mazagan (1769), and seized the Betsey (1784). The US had no navy. Antagonizing a proven Mediterranean naval power was not an option. (3) COMMERCIAL ACCESS Mediterranean trade routes were Moroccos to grant or deny. Without the Treaty, American merchant shipping had no protection in the Atlantic-Mediterranean corridor. (4) FRENCH PRESSURE France (Senior Lienholder via 1778 Treaty) had its own rivalry with Morocco (Larache defeat). The Treaty establishes a DIRECT US-Morocco relationship that bypasses France reducing French leverage over American foreign relations. (5) PRECEDENT SETTING The Treaty was ratified July 18, 1787, two months BEFORE the Constitution was signed September 17. It was ratified DURING the Convention. Article VI (supreme Law of the Land) was written WITH THIS TREATY already in force. The Framers knew exactly what they were constitutionalizing. The Moors Sundry Act (this year) is the last legislative acknowledgment of Moorish standing before classification overwrites it. [E-W4-032-01] W4 1831 military TA MILESTONE
Cherokee Nation v. Georgia
Marshall rules the Cherokee are a 'domestic dependent nation': a State, and uniformly treated as one since the settlement of the country, but NOT a foreign state in the sense the Constitution uses that term, and therefore without standing to sue Georgia in this Court. The syllabus affirms both halves: 'The acts of our Government plainly recognise the Cherokee Nation as a State, and the Courts are bound by those acts.' Creates a new jurisdictional category: the 'ward' of the federal government, in 'a state of pupilage'. The Heir is not stripped of statehood; the Heir is stripped of the forum in which statehood could be asserted.

[E-W4-034-01] W4 1832 legal TA MILESTONE
Worcester v. Georgia
Marshall rules Cherokee Nation is sovereign territory where Georgia law has no force: 'a distinct community occupying its own territory, with boundaries accurately described, in which the laws of Georgia can have no force, and which the citizens of Georgia have no right to enter'. The ruling is not enforced. THE LINE LONG ATTRIBUTED TO JACKSON, that Marshall has made his decision and may now enforce it, IS APOCRYPHAL: it is not in the judgment, it is sourced nowhere here, and the scholarship treats it as invention. What is documented is the non-enforcement itself, which the corpus sources where it actually happened, in Scott's 1838 removal address. The Software exists; the Hardware (military force) overrides it.

[E-W4-035-01] W4 1832 information TC MILESTONE
Rafinesque: Primitive Black Nations of America
NEW (W4-010): Professor Constantine Samuel Rafinesque publishes 'The Primitive Black Nations of America' in Atlantic Journal and Friend of Knowledge. The piece is a notice of an award, not the research: the Society of Geography in Paris awarded a gold medal of 100 francs for two Memoirs, communicated to him by Warden, former US Consul in Paris, and Jomard of the Institute, and Rafinesque states that the details are 'given at length, proved by authorities and compared Vocabularies, in my Memoir'. That Memoir is not held by this corpus. What the article carries is an enumeration of twelve black nations he claims to trace in North and South America, and his own language is contemptuous throughout: item 4 gives 'the black Aroras of Raleigh, or Faruras of the Spaniards, ugly black or brown Negroes, yet existing near the Oronoco, and language known, called Monkeys by their neighbours'; item 7 gives 'the Nigritas of Martyr in Darien, yet existing in Choco under the name of Chuanas or Gannas or Chinos... Ugly black or red Negroes'; item 10 gives 'the Enslen or Esteros of New California, ugly blackish Negroes'. THE FORENSIC KEY: a European scientist, rewarded by a Paris learned society, is enumerating melanated aboriginal populations in print at the same moment the U.S. is spending three wars and $40 million (Seminole Wars, running concurrently) to reclassify and destroy them. The science records the Noun in the same breath that it degrades it; the military enforces the Adjective. They run simultaneously. The military wins.

The Fichte Mann Carlisle chain: the education technology crosses from European military context to American civilian context to boarding school deployment against indigenous children. Technology 13 operational. Chain forward: Massachusetts passes first compulsory attendance law 1852 (W5 opening year). Tier A (Manns annual reports are standard educational history).

[E-W4-040-01] W4 1841 legal TA MILESTONE

Supreme Court rules the Mende captives are FREE because they were illegally enslaved under international treaty (the Anglo-Spanish treaty banning the slave trade). John Quincy Adams argues: the captives were never legally property because the underlying instrument (slave trade ban) voided their classification. If treaty law can void the classification for the Mende, it can void it for the Moors. [E-W4-041-01] W4 1841b legal TA MILESTONE
Creole Mutiny JURISDICTIONAL NAVIGATION AT SEA
November. Enslaved people aboard the American brig Creole revolt, seize control of the ship, and sail to Nassau, British Bahamas. Britain, having abolished slavery in 1833, frees 128 of the 135 classified people. The US demands their return. Britain refuses. Most successful maritime revolt in US history. TWO MARITIME REVOLTS IN THE SAME YEAR: Amistad and Creole together prove the pattern the classification system breaks at MARITIME JURISDICTIONAL SEAMS. Amistad navigates US/Spanish treaty law. Creole navigates US/British jurisdictional boundaries. The Noun escapes the Adjective by crossing legal boundaries at sea. THE MATRYOSHKA FRAMEWORK: Same logic as the Fort Mose/Spanish Florida sanctuary (W2/W3) but on water competing Managerial jurisdictions with different classification rules. The classified population navigates between them. THE CREOLE FREED MORE PEOPLE THAN THE AMISTAD (128 vs. 53) but is far less known itself a Lane 8 observation. The Amistad became a Supreme Court case and a Hollywood film. The Creole was settled by international arbitration (1855, $110,330 awarded to the US) and is largely absent from the standard narrative. Source: FRUS (Secretary of State Daniel Webster / Lord Ashburton diplomatic correspondence). Senate records. 1853 Claims Convention arbitration (Tier B).

Congressman Charles Hudson, on the official record, one year before the Mexican-American War, explicitly identifies the populations west of the Nueces/Rio Bravo as Mauritanian and Moorish, acknowledges that Mexico properly belongs to them, and predicts that if the races cross the boundary, one or the other race must be conquered, if not extinguished. THE FORENSIC KEY: A sitting U.S. congressman NAMES the populations the military is about to seize territory from as MOORS. This is not a retrospective analysis it is a contemporary identification on the official legislative record. Hudson connects the Seminole Wars (eastern front) to the upcoming Mexican-American War (western front) as two flanks of the same campaign against the same civilizational population. The stupendous deserts between the Nueces and the Bravo rivers are the boundary between the Anglo-Saxon and the Mauritanian races. The Managers KNOW who they are displacing. Source: Congressional Globe (Tier A official legislative record). [E-W4-049-01] W4 1816 financial TA
Second Bank of the United States chartered - the government takes one fifth
April 10, 1816. Congress incorporated the subscribers to the Bank of the United States at 3 Stat. 266, chapter XLIV, with a capital of thirty-five million dollars in 350,000 shares of one hundred dollars. Seventy thousand of those shares, seven million dollars, were subscribed and paid for by the United States itself. The remaining 280,000 were subscribed by individuals, companies and corporations. The charter expired and the Act of March 3, 1819 amended it.
sources: [A live_verified] src-second-bank-charter-1816 https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/STATUTE-3/pdf/STATUTE-3-Pg266.pdf

[E-W4-050-01] W4 1788 legal TA
United States Constitution ratified
1788. The Constitution drafted at Philadelphia in September 1787 was ratified by the ninth state, satisfying Article VII's condition that ratification by nine states suffice to establish it between the states so ratifying. The new government commenced the following year. This entry exists because two W3 dossiers reference the ratification by event_id and could not resolve it, one of them the Morocco treaty dossier, for which the treaty's pre-constitutional date is the operative point.
sources: [A live_verified] src-us-constitution-article-vii https://www.law.cornell.edu/constitution/articlevii

[E-W4-051-01] W4 1824 legal TA
Vagrancy Act 1824 - idleness as an offence
1824. The Vagrancy Act of 5 Geo. 4 c. 83 consolidated English law on rogues, vagabonds and idle and disorderly persons, making presence without visible means of subsistence a punishable status rather than an act. This entry exists because the W0 dossier on the Statute of Labourers and the W3 dossier on transport contracts both reference it by event_id as the continuation of status-based labour compulsion and could not resolve it.
sources: [A live_verified] src-vagrancy-act-1824 https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/Geo4/5/83/section/4

[E-W5-007-03] W5 1861 legal TC
Sine Die Adjournment - CONSTITUTIONAL BREAKPOINT
March 27-28. Seven Southern states walk out of the 36th Congress. Senate adjourns “sine die” - “without day.” No quorum to set reconvening date. Lincoln convenes both Houses July 4 under Article II “extraordinary Occasions.” THE DE JURE REPUBLIC DISSOLVES. Everything after 1861 operates under extraordinary/emergency authority. The Congress that reconvenes is a wartime Congress under executive command, not the original constitutional body. The Corporate Shift begins here.
sources: [A live_verified] src-muscatine-journal-sine-die-motion-1861 http://www.loc.gov/resource/sn84027253/1861-03-29/ed-1/?sp=2
links: dred-scott-press-coverage-1857, 160-acres-free-homestead-1862, 13th-amendment-1865

[E-W5-011-02] W5 1865 legal TA
13th Amendment
Bureau of Refugees, Freedmen, and Abandoned Lands (1865-1872). Creates the LARGEST DOCUMENTATION of classified people’s identities, property, marriages, labor contracts, and land claims in American history. Marriage records, apprenticeship records, labor contracts, complaints, land leases, hospital records. This is the single largest counter-archive to Plecker’s 1924 overwrite. FORENSIC QUESTION: what survived? Bureau records transferred to the War Department after abolition (1872).
sources: [A live_verified] src-13th-amendment-1865 https://www.archives.gov/founding-docs/amendments-11-27

[E-W5-016-04] W5 1871 legal TB
DC Organic Act - Republic becomes Corporation
41st Congress, Session III, Chapter 62, p. 419. Constitutes the District 'a body corporate for municipal purposes' (16 Stat. 419). The word 'private' is not in the statute; reading this incorporation as the Republic becoming a Corporation is the corpus's inference. Sine Die (1861) dissolved the de jure Republic; the Organic Act (1871) installs the Corporation. Ten years between death of one entity and birth of another. “Constitution OF” replaces “Constitution FOR.” The 14th Amendment “citizens” (1868) are now subjects of a corporation, not a republic.
sources: [B live_verified] src-dc-organic-act-1871 https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Page:United_States_Statutes_at_Large_Volume_16.djvu/453
links: 400-patents-held-by-black-inventors-1900, oklahoma-statehood-1907, garrett-morgan-traffic-signal-patent-1923
threads: T-FRANCHISE-TRANSFER

[E-W5-022-01] W5 1877 legal TB
Hayes-Tilden Compromise
Disputed 1876 election resolved by Electoral Commission (19 Stat. 227). Hayes gets presidency; Reconstruction ENDS. Federal troops withdrawn from the South. The classified population is abandoned. The deal: the Managers trade enforcement of the 14th/15th Amendments for control of the executive. The “protection” was always contingent on political convenience.

[E-W5-027-03] W5 1887 legal TB
Dawes Act - Allotment + Classification
86M Native acres taken. But the ACT is only half the weapon. The DAWES ROLLS (enrollment lists, 1898-1914) are a classification engine IDENTICAL to Plecker’s. The Dawes Commission decides blood quantum, assigns identity, determines land access. People who refuse to enroll LOSE their land. People who enroll get allotments that are then swindled via Burke Act (1906) “competency” determinations.

[E-W5-028-05] W5 1889 certification TC
Pan-American Commercial Conference
1889-1890. The corpus reads the Conference as evidence that Central and South American states operated under treaty relations and Iberian-derived title. The invitation text sourced here records the convening and its commercial agenda, not that finding. FEAR: if Northern Heirs (“Negroes”) realize connection to Southern Sovereigns, they could form a Continental Trust dwarfing the U.S. Corporation. The Conference proves the 1787 Treaty has hemispheric implications.
sources: [B live_verified] src-evening-bulletin-american-conference-invitation-1889 http://www.loc.gov/resource/sn87060190/1889-10-03/ed-1/?sp=1

[E-W5-037-03] W5 1900 innovation TC
400+ patents held by Black inventors - assignment vs. retention
Henry E. Baker at Patent Office compiles comprehensive list. 1899: G.F. Grant golf tee patent. FORENSIC QUESTION: how many patents were ASSIGNED to corporate buyers vs. RETAINED by the inventor? Granville Woods won against Edison twice in patent interference cases - but Woods died in 1910, impoverished, having sold most of his patents to corporate buyers (GE, Westinghouse, AT&T) for fractions of their commercial value.

[E-W5-041-04] W5 1906 sovereign TC
Algeciras Conference
13 nations (including the U.S.) meet to discuss Morocco’s sovereignty. Thirteen nations conferring on Morocco's status is the record. The sourced continuity is narrower: the 1787 Treaty of Peace and Friendship remains in force, the longest continuously in-force treaty of the United States (E-W4-002-01). The U.S. is forced to acknowledge the 1787 Treaty on a GLOBAL stage. Same decade Drew Ali begins organizing. The Noun’s international standing is being foreclosed at the exact moment the Noun’s domestic advocate appears.

[E-W5-046-03] W5 1913 counter-move TC MILESTONE
★ Noble Drew Ali begins organizing - the Noun recovered
Moorish Science Temple of America. Asserts “Moor” (Noun) against “Negro” (Adjective). Falls completely outside press framework. SAME YEAR as the Collection Engine (Federal Reserve + IRS). Organizational structure: temples in Newark (1913), Chicago, Detroit, Philadelphia, Pittsburgh, Harlem - 30+ cities by 1929. Membership estimates: 20,000-100,000. Key documents: the Circle Seven Koran, nationality cards (“Moorish American”), and the 1790 Moors Sundry Act itself.
sources: [A live_verified] src-richmond-planet-msta-charter-1928 http://www.loc.gov/resource/sn84025841/1928-05-26/ed-1/?sp=1

[E-W5-047-01] W5 1914 financial TB
NYSE closed 4 months
Closed 31 July 1914, with stock trading resuming in stages from 12 December: the longest closure in the exchange's history. New York was the LAST major exchange to suspend, every other important exchange having closed the same day. WWI triggers capital flight. The financial system literally shuts down while the new Fed takes control
sources: [A live_verified] src-washington-times-nyse-closed-1914 http://www.loc.gov/resource/sn84026749/1914-07-31/ed-1/?sp=1
links: 400-patents-held-by-black-inventors-1900, jekyll-island-meeting-secret-1910, noble-drew-ali-begins-organizing-the-1913, wall-street-bombing-1920
threads: T-MERCHANT-BANKER-CONSTANT

[E-W5-047-02] W5 1914 innovation TB
Madam C.J. Walker, widely called the first female self-made millionaire
Front-page coverage in The Freeman of Indianapolis, September 1913, carries Walker as a national business figure urging Black women into business: 'THE NEGRO WOMAN IN BUSINESS. Mme. C. J. Walker Urges her Sisters to Rise Above the Wash Tub and Cook Kitchen'. The 'first female self-made millionaire' description is the one attached to her in the press and in later biography; IT IS NOT SOURCED HERE, no wealth figure appears in the coverage, and the priority claim is contested in the scholarship on its own terms. Walker's company records at the Indiana Historical Society are the route to settling it. Note also that the coverage is September 1913 and this entry sits at 1914, a one-year displacement handled under the standing placement policy.
sources: [B live_verified] src-freeman-walker-negro-woman-in-business-1913 http://www.loc.gov/resource/sn82016211/1913-09-20/ed-1/?sp=1
links: mckinley-assassination-dominates-1901, algeciras-conference-1906, garrett-morgan-traffic-signal-patent-1923

[E-W5-062-01] W5 1922 legal TA
Ozawa v. United States - white means Caucasian
November 13, 1922. The Supreme Court held that Takao Ozawa, born in Japan, was ineligible for naturalisation because the statutory phrase 'free white person' means, in the Court's words, 'only a person of what is popularly known as the Caucasian race'. Ozawa was found to be 'clearly of a race which is not Caucasian and therefore belongs entirely outside the zone on the negative side'. The statute construed is the free-white-persons limitation retained in the Naturalization Act of 1870.
sources: [A live_verified] src-ozawa-v-united-states-1922 https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/260/178

[E-W5-063-01] W5 1923 legal TA
United States v. Thind - Caucasian is not what white means
February 19, 1923. Three months after Ozawa, the Supreme Court held Bhagat Singh Thind ineligible for naturalisation although he was argued to be Caucasian. The Court reasoned that 'the words white persons are words of common speech', to be read by common understanding rather than scientific definition, and that 'Caucasian is a conventional word of much flexibility' whose popular sense is narrower than its scientific one. Ozawa was excluded for not being Caucasian; Thind was excluded because Caucasian is not what the statute means.
sources: [A live_verified] src-us-v-thind-1923 https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/261/204

[E-W5-064-01] W5 1861 military TA
American Civil War begins

[E-W6-001-01] W6 1921 financial TB
Sheppard-Towner Maternity Act - BIRTH REGISTRATION AS FINANCIAL INSTRUMENT
November 23, 1921. First federal social welfare program. Provides FEDERAL FUNDING to states for maternal and infant health. THE MECHANISM: to receive federal funds, states must establish UNIFORM BIRTH REGISTRATION. By 1929, every state has a standardized birth certificate system. THE FORENSIC READING: The Act is Layer 2 of the Matryoshka. The 14th Amendment (1868) created the jus soli citizen-class. The 1921 Act creates the DOCUMENTATION SYSTEM that registers every person born into that class. The birth certificate is not a health document - it is a financial instrument. It creates a “legal person” (the UPPERCASE name on the certificate) distinct from the living being. That legal person is a MUNICIPAL BOND - a revenue-generating unit within the Corporate ledger. Each registered birth creates a future taxpayer, debtor, and collateral unit. The 14th Amendment supplies the constitutional authority. The 1921 Act supplies the registration mechanism. The 1933 bankruptcy (HJR 192) supplies the moment when the registered citizen-class becomes the SOLE COLLATERAL for national debt. CHAIN: 14th Amendment (1868, citizen-class created) → 1870 Naturalization Act (jus soli overwrite) → 1871 DC Organic Act (Corporation installed) → 1913 Federal Reserve (Collection Engine) → HERE (registration mechanism) → 1924 Plecker (racial classification on birth certificates) → 1933 HJR 192 (citizen-class becomes debt collateral) → 1935 SSN (permanent individual identifier). The pipeline is complete by 1935. Every American born after 1921 enters the Corporate ledger at birth.
sources: [B live_verified] src-sheppard-towner-act-1921 https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Page:United_States_Statutes_at_Large_Volume_42_Part_1.djvu/252

[E-W6-001-02] W6 1921 population TB
Universal birth registration operationalized
Before 1921, birth registration is inconsistent - many births (especially rural, Black, indigenous) go unregistered. Birth registration standardises across the period. By 1929 the registration area covered 46 of the 48 states, the Census report stating 'for the 46 states in the registration area in 1929', with Nevada and New Mexico admitted that year: NEAR-NATIONAL, NOT COMPREHENSIVE. The Maternity Act is not the instrument that did this; 'birth registration', 'registration' and 'birth' all return ZERO in its text. Plecker’s Racial Integrity Act (1924) piggybacks on this infrastructure: the birth certificate that registers the citizen also classifies the citizen by race. The registration mechanism and the classification mechanism are the SAME DOCUMENT.

[E-W6-002-01] W6 1933 financial TA
BANKRUPTCY #3 - HJR 192, Gold Seizure & McFadden Charges
THREE INSTRUMENTS, SAME YEAR: (1) Executive Order 6102 (April 5): All citizens must surrender gold at $20.67/oz. Government revalues to $35/oz - 69% overnight wealth seizure. (2) House Joint Resolution 192 (June 5): SUSPENDS THE GOLD STANDARD. Suspends citizens’ right to demand payment in gold. All debts become payable in Federal Reserve Notes (debt-backed paper) instead of lawful money (gold/silver). Common law (substance-based contracts) is effectively suspended. The people’s labor becomes the SOLE COLLATERAL for perpetual debt. The Secretary of the Treasury becomes the RECEIVER of the bankrupt estate. (3) The Emergency Banking Act (March 9): Amends the Trading with the Enemy Act (1917) to include US CITIZENS as the “enemy.” The government’s own citizens are reclassified as adversaries of the bankrupt estate. THE THREE BANKRUPTCIES: #1 (1789) - Constitution as debt reorganization, First Bank as receiver. #2 (1861) - Sine Die, de jure Republic dissolves, 14th Amendment creates new collateral base. #3 (1933) - HJR 192, gold seized, citizen-labor becomes sole collateral. The 1787 Treaty survives ALL THREE. International law instruments are not dissolved by the bankruptcy of one party. The Corporation has been bankrupt three times. The Heirs’ standing is not.
sources: [A live_verified] src-usc-31-5118-gold-clauses https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/31/5118

Congressman Louis T. McFadden, CHAIRMAN of the House Banking and Currency Committee, brings formal charges on the floor of Congress against the Federal Reserve Board of Governors: conspiracy, fraud, unlawful conversion, and TREASON. The birth certificates (1921 Act) are the REGISTRATION. The 14th Amendment (1868) is the AUTHORITY. HJR 192 (1933) is the ACTIVATION. [E-W6-003-01] W6 1932 population TA
Tuskegee Syphilis Study begins - Rebrand disproof

Runs from 1932 to 1972 - 40 YEARS. Survives the New Deal, WWII, the Civil Rights Act (1964), and the Voting Rights Act (1965) WITHOUT BEING STOPPED. The Rebrand didn’t touch it. The study continued for SEVEN YEARS after the Civil Rights Act. If you need one data point proving the 1964 legislation changed the Adjective’s label without changing the operational logic, Tuskegee is it. Penicillin becomes standard treatment for syphilis by 1947 - the study continues for 25 more years. Exposed by Peter Buxtun to the AP in 1972 - Lane 8 finally breaks through.

[E-W6-004-01] W6 1934 financial TB
National Housing Act / FHA - Geographic Classification
Creates the Federal Housing Administration. Home Owner’s Loan Corporation (HOLC) draws REDLINING MAPS: every neighborhood in America color-coded by “risk.” Red = “hazardous” = Black. The classification migrates from PEOPLE to GEOGRAPHY. The Adjective is mapped onto the physical landscape. FHA refuses to insure mortgages in red zones. Banks refuse to lend. The result: the largest wealth-building mechanism in American history (homeownership) is systematically denied to the classified population. This is the New Deal’s classification infrastructure: Social Security numbers (1936), FHA maps (1934), WPA employment records. The federal government rebuilds the classification system with NEW DATABASES after the old paper trail (courthouses, Freedmen’s Bureau) has been damaged or destroyed. chain: Lloyd’s policy (W2) → SSC bond (W3) → Zong insurance claim (W3) → HERE (geographic classification) → subprime MBS (2008) → 23andMe genetic risk (2007). Classification → risk pricing → geographic exclusion → wealth extraction.

[E-W6-004-02] W6 1934 land TC

The 2008 subprime crisis is downstream of these maps.

[E-W6-016-01] W6 1967 legal TC
Utah Memorial - 14th Amendment ratification formally challenged
Utah State Legislature passes a formal memorial (joint resolution) to Congress challenging the legality of the 14th Amendment’s ratification. THE ARGUMENT: the amendment was never lawfully ratified because (1) Southern states were under military occupation and had no legitimate legislatures capable of free consent; (2) states that initially rejected the amendment were coerced into ratification as a condition of readmission to the Union; (3) Secretary of State Seward’s 1868 proclamation acknowledged the irregularities but certified ratification anyway; (4) Ohio and New Jersey formally RESCINDED their ratifications before the final count, but were counted regardless. The Utah Memorial concludes: the 14th Amendment was imposed by force, not adopted by consent. A STATE LEGISLATURE formally tells Congress: the Matryoshka’s foundation is fraudulent. Congress does not respond. The Memorial remains in the record, unaddressed - like McFadden’s charges (1933). FORENSIC SIGNIFICANCE: if the 14th Amendment was never lawfully ratified, then: (1) the jus soli citizen-class it created has no constitutional basis; (2) the DC Organic Act (1871) that depends on it has no constitutional basis; (3) the collateral base for the 1933 bankruptcy has no constitutional basis; (4) every layer of the Matryoshka collapses. [E-W6-031-01] W6 2020 legal TA
McGirt v. Oklahoma - the reservation was never disestablished
July 9, 2020. The Supreme Court held 5-4 that the Muscogee (Creek) Nation reservation in eastern Oklahoma was never disestablished by Congress, so roughly the eastern half of the state remains Indian country for purposes of the Major Crimes Act. Gorsuch, writing for the majority, opened on the premise that only Congress can disestablish a reservation and that it had not done so here. The decision reaches the Oklahoma Enabling Act of 1906 and the allotment statutes that W5 sources, and it reaffirmed Worcester v. Georgia at length.
sources: [A live_verified] src-mcgirt-v-oklahoma-2020 https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/18-9526

[E-W6-031-02] W6 2020 land TA
Eastern Oklahoma remains Indian country
The holding did not transfer land. It established that a jurisdictional boundary the state had treated as extinguished for over a century remained in force, which is a finding about the PERSISTENCE of a treaty line rather than about ownership. Subsequent litigation narrowed the criminal jurisdiction consequences without disturbing the boundary finding.
sources: [A live_verified] src-mcgirt-v-oklahoma-2020 https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/18-9526

[E-W6-032-01] W6 1965 legal TA
Immigration and Nationality Act of 1965 - national origins repealed
October 3, 1965. The Hart-Celler Act abolished the national-origins quota system installed by the Immigration Act of 1924, which W5 sources at 43 Stat. 153. It replaced country-of-birth quotas with hemispheric caps and a preference system built on family reunification and occupational skill. The 1924 arithmetic of two percent of the 1890 census, and the 1921 three percent of 1910 before it, ceased to govern admission.
sources: [A live_verified] src-usc-8-1152-immigration-1965 https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/8/1152

[E-W6-032-02] W6 1965 population TB
The quota system ends, the classification does not
Repeal of national origins removed the explicit country-of-birth ranking. It did not remove the administrative categories through which admission is recorded, and the corpus tracks those categories rather than the quotas as such. This entry exists because construct-whitening-project and construct-admin-patch-cycle both name this Act as a member and neither could resolve it.
sources: [A live_verified] src-usc-8-1152-immigration-1965 https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/8/1152

[E-W6-033-01] W6 1983 population TB
Corrections Corporation of America founded - custody as a contract
1983. CCA, later CoreCivic, was incorporated in Nashville and is generally identified as the first modern private prison corporation in the United States. GEO Group, founded the following year as Wackenhut Corrections, became the second. Custody of prisoners returned to a per-head contractual footing for the first time since the convict-leasing systems W5 records at 1882.
sources: [A live_verified] src-corecivic-10k-2025 https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1070985/000119312526060669/cxw-20251231.htm

[E-W6-033-02] W6 1983 financial TA
Per-head payment returns
The corpus points at this event from dossier-tc-017-forward, the 1718 Jonathan Forward Treasury contract, which it treats as the archetype of the sole-proprietor transport contractor paid per head under state authorisation. The forensic claim being tracked is the RETURN of that payment structure, not a continuity of institutions.
sources: [A live_verified] src-corecivic-10k-2025 https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1070985/000119312526060669/cxw-20251231.htm

[E-W6-034-01] W6 2010s population TB
Track C in the present - contracted custody and labour
The corpus's three-track labour model assigns Track C to convict transport and contracted penal labour, running from the Transportation Act of 1718 through the convict-leasing period. This entry is the model's modern terminus: prison labour under the Thirteenth Amendment's punishment exception, which W5 records as unrepealed, operated substantially through contracted facilities. Referenced by dossier-tc-015-transport.

[E-W6-035-01] W6 2010s population TB
Platform contracting - work without employment status
Through the 2010s, platform-mediated work expanded a category of labour classified as independent contracting rather than employment, which excludes it from minimum wage, overtime, unemployment insurance and collective bargaining protections. construct-three-track-labor names this as a member; the construct's interest is the CLASSIFICATION boundary rather than the technology.
sources: [A live_verified] src-dol-independent-contractor-rule-2024 https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2024-01-10/pdf/2024-00067.pdf

[E-W6-036-01] W6 1980s financial TC
IMF structural adjustment - conditionality as debt instrument
Through the 1980s the International Monetary Fund and World Bank conditioned lending to indebted states on structural adjustment programmes: currency devaluation, public spending reduction, privatisation and trade liberalisation. dossier-haitian-indemnity-1825 points here, tracking the mechanism by which sovereign debt service is made to govern domestic policy, which is the claim the 1825 indemnity established at 150 million francs.
sources: [A live_verified] src-imf-2018-review-conditionality https://www.imf.org/-/media/Files/Publications/PP/2019/PPEA2019012.ashx

[E-W6-037-01] W6 1974 financial TB
The ratings oligopoly - issuer-pays takes hold
During the early 1970s the major credit rating agencies shifted from a subscriber-pays model to an issuer-pays model, under which the entity being rated compensates the rater. The SEC's designation of Nationally Recognized Statistical Rating Organizations in 1975 entrenched the position of Moody's, Standard and Poor's and Fitch. construct-insurance-actuarial-sublane names this event; the corpus tracks the pricing of risk as a continuous instrument from Lloyd's forward.
sources: [A live_verified] src-credit-rating-agency-reform-act-2006 https://www.govinfo.gov/link/statute/120/1327

[E-W6-038-01] W6 2001 financial TA
Credit default swaps - insurance without insurable interest
Credit default swaps, developed through the 1990s and expanding sharply after 2000, transfer credit risk for a fee without requiring the buyer to hold the underlying obligation. The Commodity Futures Modernization Act of 2000 placed them outside both insurance regulation and commodities regulation. construct-insurance-actuarial-sublane names this event as a member of the actuarial chain.
sources: [A live_verified] src-cfma-2000-114-stat-2763 https://www.govinfo.gov/link/statute/114/2763

[E-W6-039-01] W6 2010s financial TB
Algorithmic risk scoring at population scale
The FICO score, whose origin W6 records at 1956, became through the 2010s one input among many in automated scoring systems reaching credit, insurance pricing, tenancy screening and employment checks. construct-insurance-actuarial-sublane names this as the chain's present terminus. The corpus's interest is that a risk score assigns a category to a person by formula, which is the actuarial operation it tracks from Lloyd's forward.
sources: [A live_verified] src-frb-credit-scoring-report-2007 https://www.federalreserve.gov/boarddocs/rptcongress/creditscore/creditscore.pdf

[E-W6-040-01] W6 2010s population TC
Consumer genetic testing at scale - ancestry as a product
Direct-to-consumer genetic testing expanded through the 2010s to tens of millions of participants. Ancestry results are reported against REFERENCE POPULATIONS assembled by each company, so the categories returned to a customer are the company's classification scheme rather than a fact about the genome. construct-whitening-project and construct-classification-franchise-protection both name this event; W6 separately records 23andMe at 2007, its retail launch year, and the A00 haplogroup at 2013; the company's own 10-K states that its accounting predecessor 'was incorporated in Delaware in 2006', so incorporation and launch are different years and the corpus means the launch.

The reference population is the classification
A test reports proximity to a reference panel. Where that panel is drawn determines what a result can say, and panels have been assembled unevenly by region and population. This entry exists because two constructs point at it and neither could resolve.

[E-W6-041-01] W6 2023 financial TB
Regional bank failures - Silicon Valley Bank and successors
March 2023. Silicon Valley Bank failed following a deposit run, the second largest bank failure in United States history at the time, followed within days by Signature Bank and in May by First Republic. Regulators invoked the systemic risk exception to guarantee uninsured deposits. dossier-tc-020-bubble points here from the South Sea Bubble, tracking the recurrence of a failure pattern rather than asserting a causal chain.

[w3-1652-counter-move] W3 1652 counter-move TC
Baptist and Quaker moral opposition documented as motivation for the 1652 Providence-Warwick statute (interpretive)
The Providence and Warwick towns had significant Baptist and Quaker populations whose religious teaching opposed slavery. Passage of the 1652 statute proves the political will existed at the town-assembly level. Non-enforcement proves the merchant-banker network (Tier 1 via Newport trade) prevailed over Tier 4 colonial elite dissent. This is the Chesapeake Class Split pattern (FW-011) executing on RI's colonial hierarchy thirty years before the 1682 Virginia race code that begins the Whitening Project timeline.

[w3-1652-legal] W3 1652 legal TA
Rhode Island Providence-Warwick anti-slavery statute passed by Assembly, structurally never enforced
The RI General Assembly at Providence and Warwick passes a statute limiting servitude to ten years, described in scholarship as the first-in-nation anti-slavery statute. The two-town restriction means Newport and Portsmouth (the actual trading centers) are exempt by geography. No enforcement mechanism is written. The statute functions as legal cover, not legal prohibition. Track A of the three-track labor system operates undisturbed through Newport merchants for the entire fifty-one-year period between passage and the 1703 Assembly formal recognition of slavery.

[w3-1662-corporate] W3 5 July 1662 corporate TB
Asiento contracted to Genoese merchant bankers, with Dutch and English companies supplying the carriage
The Spanish crown contracts the asiento to Domenico Grillo and Ambrogio Lomellini, Genoese merchant bankers, for 24,000 piezas de India across seven years, about 3,430 a year. The Dutch West India Company and the English Royal Adventurers assist in supplying it. English participation in Asiento carriage therefore predates the 1713 transfer by fifty years, under a Genoese licence rather than a British one.

[w3-1701-sovereign] W3 1701 sovereign TB MILESTONE
Two crowns take quarter shareholdings in the asiento company itself, at 100,000 livres each
Philip V grants the asiento to the French Compagnie de Guinee et de l'Assiente under Jean-Baptiste du Casse, for 48,000 piezas de India across a decade. The company, founded in 1684, is reorganised in 1701 with the Spanish and French crowns each taking a quarter shareholding at 100,000 livres apiece. Both sovereigns hold capital in the operating company rather than licensing it from outside it.

[w3-1703-legal] W3 1703 legal TA MILESTONE
Rhode Island General Assembly formally recognizes slavery, structurally voids the 1652 Providence-Warwick statute
RI General Assembly acts to formally recognize slavery as lawful across the colony, ending the fifty-one-year fiction of the 1652 anti-slavery statute. This is the franchise-transition moment for Track A of the three-track labor system in Rhode Island: the classification 'Negro' now has explicit colonial statutory backing rather than customary practice. Sets the stage for the 1708 monetization statute (TC-012) that puts an actual per-head duty on imports.

[w3-1704-sovereign] W3 1704 sovereign TA
Bourbon dynasty consolidates control over Spanish crown mid-war
During the War of the Spanish Succession (1701-1714), the Bourbon claimant Philip V consolidates administrative control over the Spanish crown by 1704, though the war continues until Utrecht 1713. Bourbon control of both France and Spain (unified through family dynasty though separate crowns) sets the Tier-2 Senior-Lienholder configuration that persists through 1815. The Bourbon control of Spain is the immediate condition that makes the 1713 Utrecht Asiento reassignment to Britain legally possible: Spain-under-Bourbon-influence trades the Asiento to a competitor sovereign in exchange for territorial peace, transferring one classification franchise (chattel license) between sovereign holders.

Rhode Island L3 duty becomes recurring revenue stream funding colonial administration
The L3 duty's revenue captures approximately what would today be several hundred thousand pounds annually into RI colonial coffers, given import volumes documented in Newport merchant records. Same event as w3-1708-legal but the financial-lane consequence: the classification is now generating tax revenue for the Tier 4 colonial government, which then flows upstream to Tier 3 imperial coordination via Navigation Acts. [w3-1708-legal] W3 1708 legal TA
Rhode Island General Assembly imposes L3 per-head duty on imported blacks
RI Assembly imposes a three-pound sterling duty on every imported black person, formally monetizing Track A of the three-track labor system at the colonial statutory level. The duty is not a restriction; it is a tax revenue stream. The classification 'black' is now a taxable category in RI colonial law. This is the direct colonial-tier parallel to what Track C (Transportation Act 1717) will do to 'felon' classification ten years later at the imperial-tier level. Same code, different tier.

[w3-1713-legal] W3 26 March 1713 legal TA MILESTONE
Asiento de Negros reassigned to Britain: 144,000 piezas de India across thirty years, Track A installed
The Asiento contract is signed by the Catholic King at Madrid on 26 March 1713, moving the monopoly on delivering enslaved Africans to Spanish America from French to British hands. Article I sets the term at thirty years, commencing 1 May 1713 and determining 1 May 1743, at 4,800 piezas de India a year and 144,000 in total. Article II fixes one composite duty of thirty three and one third per pieza covering all duties whatsoever. This is where Track A of the three-track labor system installs.

[w3-1714-legal] W3 1714 legal TA MILESTONE
Rhode Island passes runaway law and restricts Indian imports as classification-franchise protection (F1 prototype)
RI Assembly restricts Indian imports because Indian servants 'daily discouraged the importing of white servants from Great Britain.' This is the prototype instance of Classification-Franchise Protection (F1 Pattern, FW-009). Not labor-market competition: the Managerial Class eliminates an ambiguous input (Indian servants with residual sovereign standing that could generate court challenges voiding the Negro classification) rather than defending the classification. Simultaneously RI installs a runaway law extending Track A enforcement into the internal geography. Both moves execute the same F1 logic. Direct forerunner of 1924 Plecker Racial Integrity Act's Moor-category elimination.

[w3-1717-legal] W3 May 1717 (Royal Assent) legal TA MILESTONE
Transportation Act 4 Geo 1 c 11 receives Royal Assent, installing Track C domestic Asiento for English poor
The Transportation Act (Piracy Act) receives Royal Assent, installing the third rail of the three-track labor system. Track C converts English poor labeled 'felon' into 7 or 14 year property-transfer contracts. The statute uses Papal benefit-of-clergy doctrine as the trigger mechanism for classification. This is the moment domestic Asiento becomes law: the English poor become tradeable property under identical logic to Track A (Asiento African trade) and Track B (Yamasee reclassification). Proves the classification code runs on race-neutral logic; only a legal category and a statute are required.

[w3-1718-eagle-legal] W3 August 23 1718 legal TA MILESTONE
Eagle Galley departs Blackwall with 106 convicts bound for Virginia and Maryland, proof-of-concept for Track C
The Eagle Galley sails from Blackwall, London, on August 23 1718 carrying 106 convicts under the 1717 Transportation Act to Virginia and Maryland. Butler's 1896 AHR paper documents the ship's manifest and destination. This is the operational proof-of-concept voyage for Track C: the Managerial Class demonstrates that the domestic Asiento can move human cargo at scale (100+ persons per voyage) using the identical logistics as the Middle Passage. The classification 'felon' has been fully operationalized as a property-transfer category.

Unlike the SSC (Track A, joint-stock corporation) and the SC colonial government (Track B, sovereign body), the Forward operation is a private sole-proprietor operation with monopoly rights. Forward becomes the Tier 5 operational contractor of Track C in exact parallel to the South Sea Company as Tier 5 operational contractor of Track A. Emperor Brim of the Coweta town of the Muskogee Creek Confederacy convenes representatives of Britain, France, and Spain at Coweta and secures a mutual neutrality doctrine binding all three imperial powers simultaneously with respect to the Creek Confederacy's territory. The doctrine holds for 45 years until the 1763 Treaty of Paris redraws imperial claims. This is the largest single sovereign counter-move documented in W3: the Muskogee Confederacy uses tri-imperial rivalry to preserve autochthonous jurisdiction against all three colonial systems at once.

[w3-1720-financial] W3 June 1720 financial TA MILESTONE
Bubble Act 6 Geo 1 c 18 legally monopolizes joint-stock form for SSC, three months before September SSC crash
The Bubble Act receives Royal Assent in June 1720, three months before the September to December 1720 SSC crash. The Act criminalizes competing joint-stock formations and legally cements the SSC monopoly. Standard academic narrative frames the Bubble Act as post-crash regulatory response; primary-source chronology proves the opposite. This is the N1 Collapse-Survival Pattern operating as preemptive legal armor: the state installs statutory protection for the classification-backed instrument BEFORE the instrument crashes, ensuring that when the bond fails the underlying Track A classification is preserved intact. Slave trading peaks in 1725, five years after the crash. The bond fails; the classification does not.

Physical settlement established two miles north of St Augustine
Fort Mose is constructed as a fortified free-Black settlement two miles north of St Augustine, Florida. Approximately 100 residents initially, growing through 1740. Physically instantiates the sanctuary policy on Spanish-controlled land within one hundred miles of the English South Carolina slave-plantation border. The physical proximity to English territory is the operational choice: it makes the counter-strategy visible and provokes English retaliation, which was the Spanish diplomatic objective.

[w3-1740-legal] W3 1740 legal TA MILESTONE
SC Negro Act codifies 58-section comprehensive slave code
South Carolina General Assembly enacts Act No. 670, comprising 58 sections of comprehensive slave code addressing movement restrictions, gathering limits, literacy prohibition, arms prohibition, and slaveholder authority. Passed as direct legislative response to the Stono Rebellion of September 1739. Establishes 'Negro' as a comprehensive legal classification, not merely a demographic descriptor. Template statute that the 1790 US Naturalization Act and 1924 Racial Integrity Act inherit and refine. Framework significance: this is the classification statute that the 1790 Moors Sundry Act tests as counter-move in Window 4.

Britain formally recognizes Moroccan sovereignty over West African littoral
Treaty of Peace and Commerce between Great Britain and Morocco 1750 acknowledges the Emperor of Morocco's sovereign standing over 'all the West of Africa.' This is Britain's sovereign admission against interest, made 73 years before Johnson v M'Intosh (1823) attempts to install the Discovery Doctrine as US federal law. The treaty text preserves the pre-1823 documentary record that the sovereignty framework of the American Levant contains a bilateral treaty acknowledgment that the Discovery Doctrine premise is factually false at the West African end of the trans-Atlantic trade chain.

[w3-1751-information] W3 1751 information TA
Publication as instrument of demographic-policy legitimation
The 1755 Boston publication of Observations Concerning the Increase of Mankind converts Franklin's private manuscript into a public policy artifact. Reprinted in London, Edinburgh, Philadelphia, and elsewhere through the 1760s. Cited by Malthus in his Essay on the Principle of Population 1798 as founding demographic-engineering source. The information-architecture significance is that the essay becomes the citable authority for subsequent whitening-project legislation, converting Franklin from private commentator to legitimating source.

[w3-1751-population] W3 1751 population TA MILESTONE
Franklin names the Whitening Project explicitly
Benjamin Franklin drafts Observations Concerning the Increase of Mankind in 1751. The essay articulates the demographic engineering thesis: colonial North America should exclude 'all Blacks and Tawneys' in order to increase 'the lovely White.' Describes the Americas including inhabitants as 'wholly tawny' relative to a small English contingent. This is the earliest Anglo-American articulation of the Whitening Project as explicit demographic policy, not implicit outcome. The essay circulates in manuscript from 1751 and is first published in Boston in 1755. Sets the demographic-engineering framing that the 1790 Naturalization Act codifies into US federal law.
sources: [A live_verified] src-franklin-observations-1751 https://founders.archives.gov/documents/Franklin/01-04-02-0080; [A citation_only] src-wood-franklin

[w3-1755-records] W3 1755 records TA
Lisbon Earthquake destroys Portuguese Ministry of the Overseas archives
The Lisbon Earthquake and subsequent fire and tsunami on November 1, 1755, destroy the Casa da India, the Casa dos Contos, and substantial portions of the Portuguese Overseas Ministry archives. Records destroyed include original commercial and colonial documentation of the pre-1755 Portuguese Atlantic including Mazagan-era North Africa records, Brazil early colonial records, and Africa trading-station records. Records-destruction effect: competing archival documentation of Discovery-Doctrine-era Portuguese territorial and commercial claims is substantially reduced, leaving surviving documentation disproportionately in British and Spanish archives that support the Anglocentric narrative of Discovery-Doctrine inheritance.

[w3-1758-information] W3 1758 information TA MILESTONE
Linnaeus Systema Naturae 10th edition installs four-race scientific taxonomy
Carl Linnaeus publishes the 10th edition of Systema Naturae in 1758, introducing the formal four-race taxonomy of Homo sapiens: europaeus (white, sanguine), americanus (red, choleric), asiaticus (yellow, melancholic), afer (black, phlegmatic). This is the taxonomic overlay that converts Franklin's 1751 demographic-engineering premise into scientific authority. The classifications carry temperamental and behavioral attributions attached to physical descriptors, installing the 'race as inherent essence' premise into the leading scientific framework of the 18th century. Downstream: Blumenbach 1795 five-race taxonomy inherits and refines; 19th c anthropometry inherits and refines; 1924 immigration quotas invoke as scientific basis; modern population genetics inherits the reference-population architecture.
sources: [A live_verified] src-linnaeus-systema-1758 https://www.biodiversitylibrary.org/item/10277; [A citation_only] src-gould-mismeasure

[w3-1758-population] W3 1758 population TA
Taxonomic codification converts demographic policy into scientific classification
Linnaeus's four-race taxonomy operates as scientific-authority legitimation of the demographic-policy framing Franklin's 1751 essay had articulated. Once populations are classified taxonomically, statute-level classification (SC Negro Act 1740, 1790 Naturalization Act) can invoke scientific taxonomy as authoritative basis. The classification code (V2.1 §II.4 Software Neutrality) obtains a scientific-authority overlay that survives subsequent challenges by relocating the classification from political to biological ground.

[w3-1763-land] W3 1763 land TA MILESTONE
Continental cession consolidates British sovereignty east of Mississippi
Physical territorial consequence of the 1763 treaty: British sovereignty extends from Hudson Bay to Florida, and from the Atlantic to the Mississippi. The autochthonous polities inhabiting this territory (Muscogee, Cherokee, Iroquois Confederacy, and dozens of smaller nations) receive no representation in the treaty and no cession by their own act. The territorial reassignment is between European sovereigns; the inhabitants of the ceded territory are treated as fixture rather than principal. The 1763 Royal Proclamation attempts to manage the tension by drawing a boundary at the Appalachians, but the classification of autochthonous polities as subjects of British sovereignty is now the legal frame.

[w3-1763-legal] W3 1763 legal TA MILESTONE
Treaty of Paris ends Seven Years War; France cedes North American holdings east of Mississippi to Britain
The 1763 Treaty of Paris terminates the Seven Years War (1756-1763). France cedes Canada and all territory east of the Mississippi River to Britain; Spain cedes Florida to Britain in exchange for Havana. The treaty eliminates France as an imperial counterweight to British power in North America. This is the Phase-0 Trigger event for Window 3's endgame: it terminates the multi-European-power context that made Coweta-Doctrine active neutrality operationally coherent, and it forces autochthonous polities across the eastern woodlands into a bilateral rather than multilateral diplomatic posture. Immediate downstream: 1763 Royal Proclamation, Pontiac's War 1763-1766, the political conditions for 1776 breakaway.

[w3-1765-counter-move] W3 1765 counter-move TB
Military validation of the 1750 sovereign-admission-against-interest
The Larache engagement supplies the empirical foundation missing from the 1750 treaty's paper acknowledgment: Morocco can defend the sovereignty Britain acknowledged. Under international law of the period, sovereignty claims require capacity to defend the territory claimed; the Larache defense operationalizes that requirement. Counter-move mechanism: the 1750 treaty plus the 1765 defense together produce a complete sovereignty claim (paper plus arms) that any subsequent US Discovery-Doctrine invocation must address to be sustained.

[w3-1765-military] W3 1765 military TB
French naval expedition against Larache repulsed by Moroccan forces
Sultan Mohammed III (Sidi Mohammed ben Abdellah) directs Moroccan naval and coastal defense forces to repel a French expeditionary force at Larache on the Atlantic coast. The engagement demonstrates that Morocco retains empirical military capacity to defend its sovereign territorial integrity against a European great power, three years before the more famous 1769 Anglo-Moroccan engagement at Al-Hoceima. Empirical proof that the 1750 British sovereign admission is not merely diplomatic recognition of a decayed power but recognition of an operationally functioning sovereign state.

[w3-1769-counter-move] W3 1769 counter-move TA
Territorial reversal of Discovery-Doctrine occupation
Mazagan expulsion operates as counter-move against the earliest Discovery-Doctrine settlement pattern. Portugal's claim to Mazagan rested on the 1455 Romanus Pontifex Papal Bull and the 1494 Treaty of Tordesillas. Reversing that occupation via military action, not treaty renegotiation, demonstrates the counter-strategy modality that neither the 1750 Anglo-Moroccan Treaty (paper) nor the 1765 Larache defense (defense) had proven: the sovereign can reclaim territory previously classified as Discovery-Doctrine possession. This is the 66-year Counter-Offensive's territorial-reversal round.

[w3-1769-military] W3 1769 military TA MILESTONE
Sultan Mohammed III expels Portuguese from Mazagan
Sultan Sidi Mohammed ben Abdellah completes the siege and expulsion of the Portuguese garrison from Mazagan (El Jadida) on the Atlantic coast. Portugal had held Mazagan continuously since 1502, 267 years. The expulsion terminates the last Portuguese enclave on the Moroccan Atlantic and closes a colonial-occupation lane running back to the earliest Discovery-Doctrine Papal Bulls. Empirical proof, four years after Larache 1765, that Moroccan military capacity is sufficient not only to defend but to reverse European territorial encroachment on Moroccan sovereign coast.

[w3-1772-counter-move] W3 1772 counter-move TA
Metropole-vs-colony jurisdictional division as counter-strategy
Somerset produces a jurisdictional-alternative counter-strategy distinct from the Coweta and Fort Mose precedents. Coweta 1718 used multi-European-power neutrality; Fort Mose 1738 used inter-imperial rivalry (English vs Spanish). Somerset uses intra-imperial jurisdictional division (English metropole vs colonial jurisdiction). All three counter-strategies operate on the same principle: the person does not change, the applicable jurisdiction does, and the classification that governs the person changes with jurisdiction. Empirical proof that the classification code (V2.1 §II.4 Software Neutrality) is jurisdiction-dependent, not person-dependent.

[w3-1772-legal] W3 1772 legal TA MILESTONE
Somerset v Stewart: Mansfield ruling voids chattel classification on English soil
Court of King's Bench, Lord Mansfield presiding, rules that James Somerset cannot be forcibly removed from England to be sold in Jamaica. Mansfield's ruling: no positive law of England authorizes slavery; therefore Somerset cannot be held as chattel while in English jurisdiction. The ruling does not abolish English colonial slavery, but it produces a jurisdictional-alternative counter-authority: the same person classified as chattel in Virginia is a free person in England. This is jurisdictional-alternative counter-strategy operating within a single imperial system, not through inter-imperial rivalry (Fort Mose 1738 precedent) but through metropole-vs-colony legal division.

[w3-1776-legal] W3 1776 legal TA
Break-charter operation: colonial charters voided; new sovereign entity claimed
The Declaration operates as break-charter instrument: it asserts termination of the colonial charter chain running back to the Papal Bull / English Crown source and installs a new sovereign entity claiming direct standing. Under the Discovery Doctrine framework, this transfer is impossible without Crown consent; the Declaration proceeds without such consent, creating a legal fiction that the 1783 Treaty of Paris later formalizes. Framework significance: the break-charter does not address the Discovery-Doctrine premise itself, which the new sovereign entity inherits and later formalizes into federal law via Johnson v M'Intosh 1823.

Continental Congress declares independence from Great Britain
The Continental Congress ratifies the Declaration of Independence on July 4, 1776. Read through the Trustee Hierarchy framework, this is a Tier 4 (Colonial Elite) breakaway from Tier 3 (Imperial Coordinator, Board of Trade / Privy Council) that preserves rather than terminates the underlying Tier 1 (merchant-banker) Trustee position. The Navigation-Act tariff revenue captured by British merchant-bankers pre-1776 does not vanish; it reroutes through new Tier 5 operational structures under the same Tier 1 ownership. The breakaway is administrative reorganization, not sovereign discharge. The Chesapeake Class Split (FW-011) framework applies: the same Tier 3 vs Tier 4 optimization conflict that produced the 1718 Transportation Act dispute produces the 1776 breakaway six decades later.

[w3-1777-certification] W3 1777 certification TA MILESTONE
Sultan Mohammed III issues first sovereign recognition of United States
On December 20, 1777, Sultan Sidi Mohammed ben Abdellah issues a declaration opening Moroccan ports to American shipping and de facto recognizing the United States as a sovereign entity. This is the first sovereign recognition of US independence by any foreign power. The recognition is unsolicited: no US diplomatic mission had approached the Moroccan court. Framework significance: the first foreign state to recognize American sovereignty is not a European power but the Moroccan sultanate, the same sovereign whose 1750 Anglo-Moroccan Treaty produced the 73-year documentary firewall against the Discovery Doctrine. Diplomatic precondition for the 1786-87 Treaty of Peace and Friendship.

Moroccan sovereign recognition transfers the sovereign-standing counter-authority framework across the 1776 break-charter transition. [w3-1778-financial] W3 1778 financial TA MILESTONE
Treaty of Alliance installs Bourbon Mortgage: France as Tier 2 Senior Lienholder
The Franco-American Treaty of Alliance (February 6, 1778) and its companion Treaty of Amity and Commerce commit France to military and financial support of the US independence project. Read as debt instrument under V2.1 §III.1, this is the installation moment of the Bourbon Mortgage: France (Bourbon dynasty) becomes the Tier 2 Senior Lienholder of the American Levant. French wartime subsidies and loans total roughly 48 million livres by 1783, denominated in specie and secured against future US revenue streams. The lien is not extinguished by war-ending settlements; it is transferred through subsequent franchise reassignments including the 1815 Rothschild franchise transfer.

[w3-1778-legal] W3 1778 legal TA
Treaty commits future US revenue streams as debt collateral
Treaty of Amity and Commerce Articles II, III, and V grant France most-favored-nation trade status and specific commercial privileges. Combined with the Alliance Treaty's military commitment, the arrangement establishes an implicit debt-collateral structure: French support is extended against future US revenue-stream access. The legal effect survives the war-ending 1783 Treaty of Paris; France retains commercial-privilege claims that later Federalist-era administrations must actively renegotiate (Jay Treaty 1794 context).

Second sovereign recognition (France) formalizes new-nation standing
The Treaty of Alliance constitutes France's formal sovereign recognition of the US, following Morocco's 1777 unsolicited recognition. Two sovereign recognitions within fourteen months (Morocco December 1777, France February 1778) establish the new US entity's standing in international law before the 1783 Treaty of Paris terminates British contest. The pair of recognitions represents different Trustee tiers: Morocco recognizing outside the European Trustee hierarchy; France recognizing as Tier 2 Senior Lienholder installing the Bourbon Mortgage.

[w3-1781-financial] W3 1781 financial TB
Zong: an insurance claim on people thrown overboard
November 1781. Approximately 132 enslaved people were thrown from the ship Zong, and the owners subsequently claimed on the insurance policy covering them as cargo. The resulting litigation, Gregson v. Gilbert, turned on whether the loss was recoverable under the policy. This entry exists because construct-insurance-actuarial-sublane names it as a member and the W6 dossier on credit default swaps references it as the origin of pricing risk on lives the insurer does not hold; neither could resolve it.

[w3-1781-records] W3 1781 records TB
The case record survives as an insurance dispute
What survives in the law reports is a commercial argument about recoverability under a marine policy. The people are present in the record as insured cargo, which is the documentary form the corpus tracks: the classification is not asserted in the case, it is presupposed by the pleadings.

[w3-1783-land] W3 1783 land TA
Territorial transfer without autochthonous representation
The 1783 treaty transfers sovereign standing over territory from Great Lakes to 31st parallel, from Atlantic to Mississippi. Autochthonous polities inhabiting the ceded territory (Iroquois Confederacy, Ohio Valley confederacies, Muscogee, Cherokee) receive no representation and no consent-to-cede. The territorial reassignment operates entirely between European-derived sovereigns. Downstream: the 1785-95 Northwest Indian War and 1794 Battle of Fallen Timbers demonstrate that autochthonous polities do not accept the treaty's terms; military enforcement is required to convert paper transfer into operational reality.

[w3-1783-legal] W3 1783 legal TA
Treaty of Paris ends Revolutionary War; Britain recognizes US independence
The Treaty of Paris (September 3, 1783) formally ends the American Revolutionary War. Britain recognizes US sovereignty over territory east of the Mississippi from the Great Lakes to the 31st parallel. The recognition formalizes what the 1777 Sultan recognition and 1778 French recognition had established internationally, but explicitly terminates British sovereign contest and rewires the Discovery-Doctrine inheritance chain: the US now inherits Discovery-Doctrine claims directly from British Crown transfer rather than via colonial-charter derivation. The Bourbon Mortgage installed 1778 is not extinguished by the peace settlement; it persists as continuing French claim.

[w3-1784-counter-move] W3 1784 counter-move TA
Naval leverage as counter-strategy against unarmed successor state
The Betsey seizure operates as counter-move on the same jurisdictional-alternative logic as the 1750 treaty and 1777 recognition, but adds the military-leverage dimension. Adams and Jefferson, corresponding from London and Paris respectively, treat the seizure as forcing function requiring diplomatic settlement rather than military response, which the US cannot mount. Result: the sequence from 1784 seizure to 1786-87 treaty ratification places Morocco in the leverage position at the moment US federal-law treaty framework is being installed under Article VI of the not-yet-ratified Constitution.

Pre-Constitutional treaty firewall installed
Ratification 11 months before the Constitution takes force converts the Moroccan treaty from executive-agreement status into inherited federal treaty obligation the moment the Constitution's Article VI attaches. This is jurisdictional pre-installation: the firewall exists in federal law from the instant federal law exists. Framework significance: this is the single most durable jurisdictional counter-authority in the audit period, running unbroken from 1787 to 2026.

[w3-1787-legal] W3 1787 legal TA MILESTONE
Treaty of Peace and Friendship, Morocco ratified July 18, 1787
The Confederation Congress ratifies the Treaty of Peace and Friendship with Morocco on July 18, 1787, negotiated by Thomas Barclay under Adams-Jefferson direction. The treaty is ratified before the US Constitution enters force (Constitution ratified June 21, 1788), which means the Moroccan treaty attaches to the not-yet-formed federal legal system through the Article VI Supremacy Clause pre-installation: any subsequent constitutional treaty framework must acknowledge the pre-existing Moroccan treaty as inherited obligation. This is the 1787 Treaty firewall: Moroccan sovereignty standing under the treaty is baked into US federal law before the Constitution establishes federal treaty-making authority.
